<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1872 -->
# 六级单词 1872 · 宵禁
中文：n. 宵禁
单词：###curfew###
搭配：###impose a curfew### 实行宵禁
例句：The new ###curfew###, which the council imposed after the riots, has alienated many adolescents and civic groups.
译文：议会在骚乱后实行的新宵禁，已经让许多青少年和市民团体感到疏离。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0151 -->
# 六级单词 0151 · 使疏远
中文：v. 使疏远，离间
单词：###alienate###
搭配：###feel alienated### 感到被疏远
例句：The new curfew, which the council imposed after the riots, has ###alienated### many adolescents and civic groups.
译文：议会在骚乱后实行的新宵禁，已经让许多青少年和市民团体感到疏离。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0101 -->
# 六级单词 0101 · 青少年；青春期的
中文：adj. 青少年期的；青春期的 n. 青少年
单词：###adolescent###
搭配：###adolescent behaviour### 青少年行为
例句：The new curfew, which the council imposed after the riots, has alienated many ###adolescents### and civic groups.
译文：议会在骚乱后实行的新宵禁，已经让许多青少年和市民团体感到疏离。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1873 -->
# 六级单词 1873 · 城市的；公民的
中文：adj. 市的，市民的；城市的；公民的
单词：###civic###
搭配：###civic duty### 公民义务
例句：The new curfew, which the council imposed after the riots, has alienated many adolescents and ###civic### groups.
译文：议会在骚乱后实行的新宵禁，已经让许多青少年和市民团体感到疏离。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0005 -->
# 六级单词 0005 · 缩写，缩略词
中文：n. 节略，缩写，缩短；缩略词
单词：###abbreviation###
搭配：###an abbreviation for ……### 的缩写
例句：Could you explain the ###abbreviation### in this footnote? Its meaning seems ambiguous, and the glossary doesn't include it.
译文：你能解释一下这个脚注里的缩写吗？它的意思好像很含糊，词汇表里也没有。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0006 -->
# 六级单词 0006 · 脚注
中文：n. 注脚
单词：###footnote###
搭配：###add a footnote### 加脚注
例句：Could you explain the abbreviation in this ###footnote###? Its meaning seems ambiguous, and the glossary doesn't include it.
译文：你能解释一下这个脚注里的缩写吗？它的意思好像很含糊，词汇表里也没有。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0007 -->
# 六级单词 0007 · 含糊的，模棱两可的
中文：adj. 模棱两可的，意义不明确的；含糊的
单词：###ambiguous###
搭配：###an ambiguous answer### 含糊的回答
例句：Could you explain the abbreviation in this footnote? Its meaning seems ###ambiguous###, and the glossary doesn't include it.
译文：你能解释一下这个脚注里的缩写吗？它的意思好像很含糊，词汇表里也没有。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0008 -->
# 六级单词 0008 · 词汇表，术语表

单词：###glossary###
搭配：###a glossary of terms### 术语表
例句：Could you explain the abbreviation in this footnote? Its meaning seems ambiguous, and the ###glossary### doesn't include it.
译文：你能解释一下这个脚注里的缩写吗？它的意思好像很含糊，词汇表里也没有。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0560 -->
# 六级单词 0560 · 腐蚀，锈蚀
中文：n. 腐蚀，侵蚀；锈；锈蚀
单词：###corrosion###
搭配：###prevent corrosion### 防锈
例句：Had inspectors not spotted the ###corrosion### in time, the bridge might have collapsed, burying cars under debris and causing catastrophic casualties.
译文：要不是检查人员及时发现了锈蚀，大桥可能已经垮塌，把车辆埋在废墟下，造成惨重伤亡。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0664 -->

搭配：###clear the debris### 清理残骸
例句：Had inspectors not spotted the corrosion in time, the bridge might have collapsed, burying cars under ###debris### and causing catastrophic casualties.
译文：要不是检查人员及时发现了锈蚀，大桥可能已经垮塌，把车辆埋在废墟下，造成惨重伤亡。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1874 -->

例句：Had inspectors not spotted the corrosion in time, the bridge might have collapsed, burying cars under debris and causing ###catastrophic### casualties.

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0013 -->
# 六级单词 0013 · 过度地，过分地
中文：adv. 过分，极端地；过度地
单词：###excessively###
搭配：###excessively long### 过长的
例句：Spending ###excessively### long hours on screens may impair children's cognitive development and aggravate their sleep problems.
译文：长时间过度看屏幕可能损害儿童的认知发展，并加重他们的睡眠问题。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0014 -->

中文：v. 损害，削弱
单词：###impair###
搭配：###impair one's health### 损害健康
例句：Spending excessively long hours on screens may ###impair### children's cognitive development and aggravate their sleep problems.
译文：长时间过度看屏幕可能损害儿童的认知发展，并加重他们的睡眠问题。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0015 -->

搭配：###cognitive development### 认知发展
例句：Spending excessively long hours on screens may impair children's ###cognitive### development and aggravate their sleep problems.

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0016 -->

单词：###aggravate###
搭配：###aggravate the problem### 使问题加剧
例句：Spending excessively long hours on screens may impair children's cognitive development and ###aggravate### their sleep problems.

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0029 -->
# 六级单词 0029 · 过敏的
中文：adj. 过敏的
单词：###allergic###
搭配：###be allergic to### 对……过敏
例句：Are you ###allergic### to anything? — Nuts and some cosmetics. — Then avoid this lotion. — Thanks, I'm acutely aware of the risk.
译文：你对什么过敏吗？——坚果和一些化妆品。——那就别用这瓶乳液。——谢谢，我非常清楚这个风险。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

搭配：###cosmetic surgery### 整容手术
例句：Are you allergic to anything? — Nuts and some ###cosmetics###. — Then avoid this lotion. — Thanks, I'm acutely aware of the risk.
译文：你对什么过敏吗？——坚果和一些化妆品。——那就别用这瓶乳液。——谢谢，我非常清楚这个风险。

例句：Are you allergic to anything? — Nuts and some cosmetics. — Then avoid this ###lotion###. — Thanks, I'm acutely aware of the risk.
译文：你对什么过敏吗？——坚果和一些化妆品。——那就别用这瓶乳液。——谢谢，我非常清楚这个风险。

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1877 -->
# 六级单词 1877 · 流行病；流行性的
中文：adj. 流行性的；传染的 n. 流行病；传播
单词：###epidemic###
搭配：###a flu epidemic### 流感疫情
例句：It was not until the ###epidemic### had devastated whole regions that officials began to allocate funds and deploy medical teams.
译文：直到疫情重创了整片地区，官员们才开始拨款、派出医疗队。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1878 -->

中文：v. 毁坏；摧毁；重创
单词：###devastate###
搭配：###be devastated by### 被……摧毁
例句：It was not until the epidemic had ###devastated### whole regions that officials began to allocate funds and deploy medical teams.
译文：直到疫情重创了整片地区，官员们才开始拨款、派出医疗队。

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1879 -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1881 -->
# 六级单词 1881 · 青春期
中文：n. 青春期
单词：###adolescence###
搭配：###during adolescence### 在青春期
例句：During ###adolescence###, young people often struggle with ambiguity about who they are, and adults should show compassion rather than arrogance.
译文：青春期的年轻人常常对“我是谁”感到困惑，大人应当表现出同情，而不是傲慢。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

单词：###ambiguity###
搭配：###without ambiguity### 明确地
例句：During adolescence, young people often struggle with ###ambiguity### about who they are, and adults should show compassion rather than arrogance.
译文：青春期的年轻人常常对“我是谁”感到困惑，大人应当表现出同情，而不是傲慢。

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1883 -->

中文：n. 同情；怜悯(for)
单词：###compassion###
搭配：###show compassion for### 同情
例句：During adolescence, young people often struggle with ambiguity about who they are, and adults should show ###compassion### rather than arrogance.
译文：青春期的年轻人常常对“我是谁”感到困惑，大人应当表现出同情，而不是傲慢。

例句：During adolescence, young people often struggle with ambiguity about who they are, and adults should show compassion rather than ###arrogance###.

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0081 -->
# 六级单词 0081 · 嗡嗡叫；嗡嗡声
中文：n. 嗡嗡声；嘈杂声 v. 发出嗡嗡声；发出嘈杂声；嗡嗡叫
单词：###buzz###
搭配：###buzz around### 嗡嗡地飞来飞去
例句：What's that ###buzzing###? — Just a beetle. — Beware, it might bite! — Relax, it's benign.
译文：那嗡嗡声是什么？——就是只甲虫。——当心，它可能咬人！——放心，它没有害处。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0218 -->

单词：###beetle###
搭配：###a black beetle### 一只黑甲虫
例句：What's that buzzing? — Just a ###beetle###. — Beware, it might bite! — Relax, it's benign.
译文：那嗡嗡声是什么？——就是只甲虫。——当心，它可能咬人！——放心，它没有害处。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0017 -->

搭配：###beware of### 小心
例句：What's that buzzing? — Just a beetle. — ###Beware###, it might bite! — Relax, it's benign.
译文：那嗡嗡声是什么？——就是只甲虫。——当心，它可能咬人！——放心，它没有害处。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1885 -->
# 六级单词 1885 · 无害的；良性的

搭配：###a benign tumour### 良性肿瘤
例句：What's that buzzing? — Just a beetle. — Beware, it might bite! — Relax, it's ###benign###.

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1886 -->
# 六级单词 1886 · （德、奥）总理；财政大臣；大学校长
中文：n. 总理（德、奥等的）；校长（美国某些大学的）；财政大臣
单词：###chancellor###
搭配：###the Chancellor of the Exchequer### 英国财政大臣
例句：The ###chancellor### insisted that every ministry curtail spending and defer new projects until the audit was complete.
译文：财政大臣坚持要求每个部门削减开支，推迟新项目，直到审计结束。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1887 -->

搭配：###curtail spending### 削减开支
例句：The chancellor insisted that every ministry ###curtail### spending and defer new projects until the audit was complete.

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1888 -->

搭配：###defer a decision### 推迟决定
例句：The chancellor insisted that every ministry curtail spending and ###defer### new projects until the audit was complete.

<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1889 -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1890 -->
# 六级单词 1890 · 侵蚀，腐蚀
中文：v. 侵蚀，腐蚀
单词：###erode###
搭配：###be eroded by### 被……侵蚀
例句：Heavy rains ###eroded### the barren hillside, washing mud onto the cropland and depleting the soil.
译文：暴雨冲刷了光秃秃的山坡，把泥沙冲到农田上，土壤也越来越贫瘠。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0127 -->
# 六级单词 0127 · 贫瘠的，荒芜的

单词：###barren###
搭配：###barren land### 不毛之地
例句：Heavy rains eroded the ###barren### hillside, washing mud onto the cropland and depleting the soil.
译文：暴雨冲刷了光秃秃的山坡，把泥沙冲到农田上，土壤也越来越贫瘠。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

搭配：###fertile cropland### 肥沃的农田
例句：Heavy rains eroded the barren hillside, washing mud onto the ###cropland### and depleting the soil.
译文：暴雨冲刷了光秃秃的山坡，把泥沙冲到农田上，土壤也越来越贫瘠。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1891 -->
# 六级单词 1891 · 耗尽，使枯竭
中文：v. 耗尽，使衰竭；使枯竭
单词：###deplete###
搭配：###deplete resources### 耗尽资源
例句：Heavy rains eroded the barren hillside, washing mud onto the cropland and ###depleting### the soil.
译文：暴雨冲刷了光秃秃的山坡，把泥沙冲到农田上，土壤也越来越贫瘠。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1892 -->
# 六级单词 1892 · 独木舟；划独木舟

搭配：###paddle a canoe### 划独木舟
例句：Shall we take the ###canoe### out? — Sure. Bring the binoculars; we may spot a crow or even a buffalo by the river.
译文：我们划独木舟出去吧？——好啊。带上望远镜，我们说不定能在河边看到乌鸦，甚至水牛。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0350 -->
# 六级单词 0350 · 使目眩；使赞叹
中文：v. 使眼花，耀眼；使惊奇，使倾倒；使目眩；使赞叹 n. 闪耀，耀眼
单词：###dazzle###
搭配：###a dazzling smile### 灿烂的笑容
例句：Did you see the eclipse last night? — Yes! The moon turned red above the cathedral dome. — How ###dazzling###!
译文：你看见昨晚的月食了吗？——看见了！月亮在大教堂的圆顶上方变成了红色。——太耀眼了！
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0951 -->

搭配：###a speaker of great eloquence### 雄辩的演说家
例句：Given her ###eloquence### and empirical evidence, the envoy persuaded both sides to sign the bilateral deal.
译文：鉴于她能言善辩、又有实证，这位特使说服双方签下了那份双边协议。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- /leiji-note -->

译文：汇总数据显示，滥用抗生素会削弱其疗效，医生们对这种趋势深感痛惜，正努力加以遏制。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0046 -->
# 六级单词 0046 · 减少，削弱
中文：v. 减少，(使)减小，降低；贬低，使降级，削弱
单词：###diminish###
搭配：###diminish the effect### 削弱效果
例句：Aggregate data show that the overuse of antibiotics may ###diminish### their effect, a trend doctors deplore and try to counteract.
译文：汇总数据显示，滥用抗生素会削弱其疗效，医生们对这种趋势深感痛惜，正努力加以遏制。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1922 -->
# 六级单词 1922 · 痛惜，谴责
中文：vt. 悲悼，哀叹；谴责；痛惜
单词：###deplore###
搭配：###deplore violence### 谴责暴力
例句：Aggregate data show that the overuse of antibiotics may diminish their effect, a trend doctors ###deplore### and try to counteract.
译文：汇总数据显示，滥用抗生素会削弱其疗效，医生们对这种趋势深感痛惜，正努力加以遏制。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1923 -->
# 六级单词 1923 · 抵消，对抗
中文：v. 抵消，中和，阻碍；对抗
单词：###counteract###
搭配：###counteract the effects### 抵消影响
例句：Aggregate data show that the overuse of antibiotics may diminish their effect, a trend doctors deplore and try to ###counteract###.
译文：汇总数据显示，滥用抗生素会削弱其疗效，医生们对这种趋势深感痛惜，正努力加以遏制。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0516 -->
# 六级单词 0516 · 辨别，看出
中文：v. 看出，了解，识别；辨别
单词：###discern###
搭配：###discern A from B### 分辨 A 和 B
例句：Good scientists ###discern### truth from delusion and challenge dogma rather than cling to doctrines that defy evidence.
译文：好的科学家能分辨真理与幻觉，敢于挑战教条，而不是死守违背证据的信条。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0752 -->
# 六级单词 0752 · 错觉，妄想
中文：n. 欺骗；哄骗；错觉；妄想
单词：###delusion###
搭配：###under the delusion that### 误以为
例句：Good scientists discern truth from ###delusion### and challenge dogma rather than cling to doctrines that defy evidence.
译文：好的科学家能分辨真理与幻觉，敢于挑战教条，而不是死守违背证据的信条。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1924 -->
# 六级单词 1924 · 教条，信条
中文：n. 教条，信条
单词：###dogma###
搭配：###religious dogma### 宗教教条
例句：Good scientists discern truth from delusion and challenge ###dogma### rather than cling to doctrines that defy evidence.
译文：好的科学家能分辨真理与幻觉，敢于挑战教条，而不是死守违背证据的信条。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0515 -->
# 六级单词 0515 · 教义，学说
中文：n. 信条，教条，主义；学说，原理，教诲；教义
单词：###doctrine###
搭配：###military doctrine### 军事学说
例句：Good scientists discern truth from delusion and challenge dogma rather than cling to ###doctrines### that defy evidence.
译文：好的科学家能分辨真理与幻觉，敢于挑战教条，而不是死守违背证据的信条。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1925 -->
# 六级单词 1925 · 跛行；跛行
中文：v. 蹒跚，跛行 n. 跛行 adj. 柔软的，易曲的
单词：###limp###
搭配：###walk with a limp### 一瘸一拐地走
例句：Why are you ###limping###? — I crouched to dodge a ball and bruised my ankle. — That hurts! Put ice on it.
译文：你怎么一瘸一拐的？——我蹲下躲球，把脚踝撞青了。——好疼！拿冰敷一下。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1926 -->
# 六级单词 1926 · 蹲伏
中文：v. 蜷缩，蹲伏
单词：###crouch###
搭配：###crouch down### 蹲下
例句：Why are you limping? — I ###crouched### to dodge a ball and bruised my ankle. — That hurts! Put ice on it.
译文：你怎么一瘸一拐的？——我蹲下躲球，把脚踝撞青了。——好疼！拿冰敷一下。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0762 -->
# 六级单词 0762 · 躲闪，躲避
中文：v. 躲开，闪开；躲避，施计逃避义务(责任等) n. 躲闪，躲避；妙计，伎俩
单词：###dodge###
搭配：###dodge a question### 回避问题
例句：Why are you limping? — I crouched to ###dodge### a ball and bruised my ankle. — That hurts! Put ice on it.
译文：你怎么一瘸一拐的？——我蹲下躲球，把脚踝撞青了。——好疼！拿冰敷一下。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1927 -->
# 六级单词 1927 · 使青肿；青肿
中文：n. (人体、水果或植物等碰撞后产生的)青肿或伤痕；(感情等方面)挫伤 v. 使(皮肉)青肿，碰伤(水果、植物等)；碾碎，捣烂(水果等)；挫伤(感情等)
单词：###bruise###
搭配：###a bruised knee### 青肿的膝盖
例句：Why are you limping? — I crouched to dodge a ball and ###bruised### my ankle. — That hurts! Put ice on it.
译文：你怎么一瘸一拐的？——我蹲下躲球，把脚踝撞青了。——好疼！拿冰敷一下。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1928 -->
# 六级单词 1928 · 史诗；宏大的
中文：n. 史诗，伟大事迹 adj. 宏大的
单词：###epic###
搭配：###an epic poem### 史诗
例句：The ###epic### film depicts an ancient empire in eloquent detail, from its glorious epoch to its desolate end.
译文：这部史诗电影以生动的细节描绘了一个古代帝国，从辉煌的时代一直到凄凉的结局。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0724 -->
# 六级单词 0724 · 描绘，描述
中文：v. (用图画、文字)表示，描述，描绘
单词：###depict###
搭配：###depict a scene### 描绘场景
例句：The epic film ###depicts### an ancient empire in eloquent detail, from its glorious epoch to its desolate end.
译文：这部史诗电影以生动的细节描绘了一个古代帝国，从辉煌的时代一直到凄凉的结局。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0950 -->

中文：adj. 雄辩的，有说服力的，口才好的；意味深长的，明白显示出的；动人的
单词：###eloquent###
搭配：###an eloquent speech### 动人的演讲
例句：The epic film depicts an ancient empire in ###eloquent### detail, from its glorious epoch to its desolate end.
译文：这部史诗电影以生动的细节描绘了一个古代帝国，从辉煌的时代一直到凄凉的结局。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0785 -->
# 六级单词 0785 · 时代，纪元
中文：n. 时代，纪元，重要时期
单词：###epoch###
搭配：###a new epoch### 新纪元
例句：The epic film depicts an ancient empire in eloquent detail, from its glorious ###epoch### to its desolate end.
译文：这部史诗电影以生动的细节描绘了一个古代帝国，从辉煌的时代一直到凄凉的结局。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0364 -->
# 六级单词 0364 · 荒凉的；凄凉的
中文：adj. 荒凉的；孤独的；凄凉的 v. 使荒芜
单词：###desolate###
搭配：###a desolate landscape### 荒凉的景色
例句：The epic film depicts an ancient empire in eloquent detail, from its glorious epoch to its ###desolate### end.
译文：这部史诗电影以生动的细节描绘了一个古代帝国，从辉煌的时代一直到凄凉的结局。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1929 -->
# 六级单词 1929 · 档案；档案馆
中文：n. 档案馆，档案室；档案
单词：###archive###
搭配：###national archives### 国家档案馆
例句：The ###archive### of the famous author contains letters, an autobiography draft and a bibliography compiled by her apprentice.
译文：这位著名作家的档案里有书信、一份自传草稿，以及她的学徒编的一份书目。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1930 -->
# 六级单词 1930 · 自传
中文：n. 自传
单词：###autobiography###
搭配：###write an autobiography### 写自传
例句：The archive of the famous author contains letters, an ###autobiography### draft and a bibliography compiled by her apprentice.
译文：这位著名作家的档案里有书信、一份自传草稿，以及她的学徒编的一份书目。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0159 -->
# 六级单词 0159 · 书目，参考书目
中文：n. 书目，目录，参考书目
单词：###bibliography###
搭配：###a full bibliography### 完整的参考书目
例句：The archive of the famous author contains letters, an autobiography draft and a ###bibliography### compiled by her apprentice.
译文：这位著名作家的档案里有书信、一份自传草稿，以及她的学徒编的一份书目。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1931 -->
# 六级单词 1931 · 编辑，汇编
中文：v. 编辑，汇编，编纂
单词：###compile###
搭配：###compile a list### 编制清单
例句：The archive of the famous author contains letters, an autobiography draft and a bibliography ###compiled### by her apprentice.
译文：这位著名作家的档案里有书信、一份自传草稿，以及她的学徒编的一份书目。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1932 -->
# 六级单词 1932 · 学徒
中文：n. 学徒 v. 当学徒
单词：###apprentice###
搭配：###an apprentice carpenter### 木匠学徒
例句：The archive of the famous author contains letters, an autobiography draft and a bibliography compiled by her ###apprentice###.
译文：这位著名作家的档案里有书信、一份自传草稿，以及她的学徒编的一份书目。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0487 -->
# 六级单词 0487 · 一次性的；可支配的
中文：adj. 可任意使用的；可支配的 n. 一次性物品，不回收物品
单词：###disposable###
搭配：###disposable income### 可支配收入
例句：###Disposable### cups seem convenient, but they clog drains, decompose slowly and are hardly conducive to a clean city.
译文：一次性杯子看似方便，却会堵塞下水道，分解缓慢，很不利于城市清洁。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1933 -->
# 六级单词 1933 · 堵塞
中文：v. 堵塞，阻塞 n. 障碍
单词：###clog###
搭配：###clog the pipes### 堵塞管道
例句：Disposable cups seem convenient, but they ###clog### drains, decompose slowly and are hardly conducive to a clean city.
译文：一次性杯子看似方便，却会堵塞下水道，分解缓慢，很不利于城市清洁。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0488 -->
# 六级单词 0488 · 分解，腐烂
中文：v. 腐败；分解；腐烂
单词：###decompose###
搭配：###decompose slowly### 缓慢分解
例句：Disposable cups seem convenient, but they clog drains, ###decompose### slowly and are hardly conducive to a clean city.
译文：一次性杯子看似方便，却会堵塞下水道，分解缓慢，很不利于城市清洁。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1934 -->
# 六级单词 1934 · 有助于……的
中文：adj. 有助于…的，有益于…的
单词：###conducive###
搭配：###be conducive to### 有助于
例句：Disposable cups seem convenient, but they clog drains, decompose slowly and are hardly ###conducive### to a clean city.
译文：一次性杯子看似方便，却会堵塞下水道，分解缓慢，很不利于城市清洁。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0612 -->
# 六级单词 0612 · 古怪的；怪人
中文：adj. 古怪的，异常的 n. 古怪的人
单词：###eccentric###
搭配：###eccentric behaviour### 古怪的行为
例句：Who's that ###eccentric### old man? — The bishop. He ignores etiquette but is very amiable.
译文：那个古怪的老头是谁？——是主教。他不讲究礼节，但人很和蔼。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1027 -->
# 六级单词 1027 · 主教
中文：n. （基督教的）主教
单词：###bishop###
搭配：###the Bishop of London### 伦敦主教
例句：Who's that eccentric old man? — The ###bishop###. He ignores etiquette but is very amiable.
译文：那个古怪的老头是谁？——是主教。他不讲究礼节，但人很和蔼。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1935 -->
# 六级单词 1935 · 礼节，礼仪
中文：n. 礼节，礼仪
单词：###etiquette###
搭配：###social etiquette### 社交礼仪
例句：Who's that eccentric old man? — The bishop. He ignores ###etiquette### but is very amiable.
译文：那个古怪的老头是谁？——是主教。他不讲究礼节，但人很和蔼。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1936 -->
# 六级单词 1936 · 和蔼可亲的
中文：adj. 和蔼可亲的，友善的，亲切的
单词：###amiable###
搭配：###an amiable person### 和蔼的人
例句：Who's that eccentric old man? — The bishop. He ignores etiquette but is very ###amiable###.
译文：那个古怪的老头是谁？——是主教。他不讲究礼节，但人很和蔼。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1937 -->
# 六级单词 1937 · 火焰，大火；燃烧
中文：n. 火，火焰；光辉，灿烂；迸发，爆发；大火 v. 燃烧，冒火焰；发光，放光彩；发怒
单词：###blaze###
搭配：###a blaze of light### 一片光亮
例句：By the time the firefighters arrived, the ###blaze### had devoured the barn and the roof had begun to crumble.
译文：消防员赶到时，大火已经吞没了谷仓，屋顶也开始坍塌。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0771 -->
# 六级单词 0771 · 吞没；狼吞虎咽
中文：v. 吞食；吞灭，毁灭；吞没；狼吞虎咽
单词：###devour###
搭配：###be devoured by fire### 被大火吞没
例句：By the time the firefighters arrived, the blaze had ###devoured### the barn and the roof had begun to crumble.
译文：消防员赶到时，大火已经吞没了谷仓，屋顶也开始坍塌。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1938 -->
# 六级单词 1938 · 谷仓，粮仓
中文：n. 谷仓；牲口棚；粮仓
单词：###barn###
搭配：###a big red barn### 一座红色大谷仓
例句：By the time the firefighters arrived, the blaze had devoured the ###barn### and the roof had begun to crumble.
译文：消防员赶到时，大火已经吞没了谷仓，屋顶也开始坍塌。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0689 -->
# 六级单词 0689 · 崩溃，坍塌
中文：v. 弄碎，粉碎；崩溃，瓦解；坍塌
单词：###crumble###
搭配：###crumble away### 逐渐崩塌
例句：By the time the firefighters arrived, the blaze had devoured the barn and the roof had begun to ###crumble###.
译文：消防员赶到时，大火已经吞没了谷仓，屋顶也开始坍塌。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1939 -->
# 六级单词 1939 · 僵局
中文：n. 僵局
单词：###deadlock###
搭配：###break the deadlock### 打破僵局
例句：The talks ended in ###deadlock### because neither side would concede, so both parties agreed to convene again and let a neutral judge arbitrate.
译文：谈判陷入僵局，因为双方都不肯让步，于是两方同意再次开会，请一位中立的法官仲裁。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1940 -->
# 六级单词 1940 · 让步；承认
中文：v. (常指勉强地)承认；容许，让与；让步
单词：###concede###
搭配：###concede defeat### 认输
例句：The talks ended in deadlock because neither side would ###concede###, so both parties agreed to convene again and let a neutral judge arbitrate.
译文：谈判陷入僵局，因为双方都不肯让步，于是两方同意再次开会，请一位中立的法官仲裁。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0452 -->
# 六级单词 0452 · 召集，召开
中文：v. 召集会议；召开
单词：###convene###
搭配：###convene a meeting### 召开会议
例句：The talks ended in deadlock because neither side would concede, so both parties agreed to ###convene### again and let a neutral judge arbitrate.
译文：谈判陷入僵局，因为双方都不肯让步，于是两方同意再次开会，请一位中立的法官仲裁。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1941 -->
# 六级单词 1941 · 仲裁，公断
中文：v. 仲裁，公断
单词：###arbitrate###
搭配：###arbitrate a dispute### 仲裁纠纷
例句：The talks ended in deadlock because neither side would concede, so both parties agreed to convene again and let a neutral judge ###arbitrate###.
译文：谈判陷入僵局，因为双方都不肯让步，于是两方同意再次开会，请一位中立的法官仲裁。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1942 -->
# 六级单词 1942 · 碰撞；冲突
中文：n. 碰撞，冲突
单词：###collision###
搭配：###a head-on collision### 迎面相撞
例句：Investigators have yet to establish whether the ###collision### resulted from a faulty cylinder or a blunder in the cockpit.
译文：调查人员尚未查明，这次相撞是因为汽缸出了故障，还是驾驶舱里有人操作失误。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0699 -->
# 六级单词 0699 · 汽缸；圆筒
中文：n. 圆筒，圆柱体；汽缸；(印刷机)滚筒；(左轮手枪的)旋转枪膛；圆柱体体积
单词：###cylinder###
搭配：###a four-cylinder engine### 四缸发动机
例句：Investigators have yet to establish whether the collision resulted from a faulty ###cylinder### or a blunder in the cockpit.
译文：调查人员尚未查明，这次相撞是因为汽缸出了故障，还是驾驶舱里有人操作失误。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0196 -->
# 六级单词 0196 · 大错；犯大错
中文：n. 错误 v. 弄错，犯大错
单词：###blunder###
搭配：###make a blunder### 犯大错
例句：Investigators have yet to establish whether the collision resulted from a faulty cylinder or a ###blunder### in the cockpit.
译文：调查人员尚未查明，这次相撞是因为汽缸出了故障，还是驾驶舱里有人操作失误。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0720 -->

搭配：###deduct tax### 扣税
例句：Can you check this bill? — They ###deducted### the fee twice! — A duplicate charge? — Yes, and there's another discrepancy: a misplaced decimal point.
译文：你能帮我看看这张账单吗？——他们扣了两次费！——重复收费？——是的，还有一处出入：小数点点错了位置。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

搭配：###a consumer boycott### 消费者抵制
例句：The activists who organized the ###boycott### claimed that the firm had ignored harassment in its affiliated factories and evaded responsibility.

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0021 -->

搭配：###a national census### 全国人口普查
例句：The latest ###census### reveals demographic shifts, such as more elderly dependants, whose cumulative effect will strain pensions.
译文：最新的人口普查显示了人口结构的变化，比如需要供养的老人越来越多，这些变化累积起来会加重养老金的负担。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

搭配：###academic credentials### 学历证明
例句：Applicants lacking the proper ###credentials### will not be eligible for clearance, however eminent they are.
译文：缺少相应资质证明的申请人，不管多有名望，都拿不到审批许可。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

搭配：###the cradle of civilization### 文明的发源地

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0698 -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0472 -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1235 -->

搭配：###grope for the light switch### 摸索电灯开关

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0906 -->

搭配：###gleam in the dark### 在黑暗中发光
例句：Fireflies, whose bodies ###gleam### in the dark, dwell in moist gorges and are a fabulous sight on summer nights.

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0857 -->

<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1959 -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1595 -->
# 六级单词 1595 · 流产，堕胎
中文：n. 流产，小产，堕胎；(计划、工程等的)中途失败；中止，中辍的计划
单词：###abortion###
搭配：###an unsafe abortion### 不安全的流产手术
例句：Doctors warn that unsafe ###abortion### is chronically underreported, as many women stay anonymous and suffer its aftermath alone.
译文：医生警告说，不安全的堕胎长期以来很少被上报，因为许多女性不愿透露身份，独自承受后果。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0429 -->

搭配：###chronically ill### 长期患病
例句：Doctors warn that unsafe abortion is ###chronically### underreported, as many women stay anonymous and suffer its aftermath alone.

<!-- /leiji-note -->

搭配：###remain anonymous### 匿名
例句：Doctors warn that unsafe abortion is chronically underreported, as many women stay ###anonymous### and suffer its aftermath alone.

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1961 -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1962 -->
# 六级单词 1962 · 皱眉；皱眉
中文：vi. 皱眉，蹙额
单词：###frown###
搭配：###frown at### 对……皱眉
例句：Why are you ###frowning###? — My phone bill doubled! — Let me flip it over... I gasp! I'd be furious too!
译文：你皱什么眉？——我的电话费翻倍了！——我翻过来看看……天哪！换我也会气坏的！
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1187 -->
# 六级单词 1187 · 轻抛；翻转
中文：v. 把…抛向空中，捻，快速地翻转(猛然移动)；大怒，强烈地反应 n. 轻弹(打、抛)
单词：###flip###
搭配：###flip a coin### 抛硬币
例句：Why are you frowning? — My phone bill doubled! — Let me ###flip### it over... I gasp! I'd be furious too!
译文：你皱什么眉？——我的电话费翻倍了！——我翻过来看看……天哪！换我也会气坏的！
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1963 -->

单词：###gasp###
搭配：###gasp in surprise### 惊得倒吸一口气
例句：Why are you frowning? — My phone bill doubled! — Let me flip it over... I ###gasp###! I'd be furious too!
译文：你皱什么眉？——我的电话费翻倍了！——我翻过来看看……天哪！换我也会气坏的！
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1964 -->

单词：###furious###
搭配：###be furious with### 对……大发雷霆
例句：Why are you frowning? — My phone bill doubled! — Let me flip it over... I gasp! I'd be ###furious### too!
译文：你皱什么眉？——我的电话费翻倍了！——我翻过来看看……天哪！换我也会气坏的！
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0626 -->
# 六级单词 0626 · 保护，保存；节约
中文：v. 节约使用，保存；保护
单词：###conserve###
搭配：###conserve energy### 节约能源
例句：Planners hope to ###conserve### historic districts and designate new zones for industry, so that a cosmopolitan city and its heritage can coexist.
译文：规划者希望保护历史街区，再划出新的工业区，让一座国际化城市与它的历史遗产共存。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0729 -->

单词：###designate###
搭配：###be designated as### 被指定为
例句：Planners hope to conserve historic districts and ###designate### new zones for industry, so that a cosmopolitan city and its heritage can coexist.
译文：规划者希望保护历史街区，再划出新的工业区，让一座国际化城市与它的历史遗产共存。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1965 -->

搭配：###a cosmopolitan city### 国际大都市
例句：Planners hope to conserve historic districts and designate new zones for industry, so that a ###cosmopolitan### city and its heritage can coexist.
译文：规划者希望保护历史街区，再划出新的工业区，让一座国际化城市与它的历史遗产共存。

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1967 -->

中文：adj. 无效的，无用的，无希望的；徒劳的
单词：###futile###
搭配：###a futile attempt### 徒劳的尝试
例句：###Futile### as it may seem, the villagers keep fortifying the riverbank, refusing to forsake their homes to their old foe, the river.
译文：尽管看起来徒劳，村民们仍在不断加固河岸，不肯把家园让给那条老冤家——河流。

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1968 -->

单词：###fortify###
搭配：###fortify a city### 加固城防
例句：Futile as it may seem, the villagers keep ###fortifying### the riverbank, refusing to forsake their homes to their old foe, the river.
译文：尽管看起来徒劳，村民们仍在不断加固河岸，不肯把家园让给那条老冤家——河流。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0603 -->

单词：###forsake###
搭配：###forsake one's home### 离乡背井
例句：Futile as it may seem, the villagers keep fortifying the riverbank, refusing to ###forsake### their homes to their old foe, the river.
译文：尽管看起来徒劳，村民们仍在不断加固河岸，不肯把家园让给那条老冤家——河流。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1971 -->
# 六级单词 1971 · 选举的
中文：adj. 选举的
单词：###electoral###
搭配：###electoral reform### 选举改革
例句：###Electoral### reforms that let citizens endorse candidates online could boost turnout among disillusioned young voters.
译文：让公民能在网上为候选人背书的选举改革，可能会提高那些幻想破灭的年轻选民的投票率。

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0028 -->

搭配：###become extinct### 灭绝
例句：As forests vanish, many species may become ###extinct###, and protecting their last haven is a formidable task, since resources are finite.
译文：随着森林消失，许多物种可能灭绝；资源有限，保护它们最后的栖息地是一项艰巨的任务。

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1071 -->

例句：People ###displaced### by war often seek asylum abroad, where they may feel alien until they assimilate into local life.
译文：因战争流离失所的人常常到国外寻求庇护，在那里他们可能一直觉得格格不入，直到融入当地生活。

例句：People displaced by war often seek ###asylum### abroad, where they may feel alien until they assimilate into local life.

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0583 -->

搭配：###take countermeasures### 采取对策

例句：Want to visit the ###casino###? — No, I'm cynical about luck. — I just crave their cakes! — Fine, let's cram in a quick visit.
译文：想去赌场看看吗？——不了，我对运气这东西没什么信心。——我就是馋他们的蛋糕！——好吧，那就抽空快去一趟。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0702 -->

搭配：###a cynical view### 悲观的看法
例句：Want to visit the casino? — No, I'm ###cynical### about luck. — I just crave their cakes! — Fine, let's cram in a quick visit.
译文：想去赌场看看吗？——不了，我对运气这东西没什么信心。——我就是馋他们的蛋糕！——好吧，那就抽空快去一趟。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1981 -->

译文：想去赌场看看吗？——不了，我对运气这东西没什么信心。——我就是馋他们的蛋糕！——好吧，那就抽空快去一趟。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1982 -->

<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0157 -->
# 六级单词 0157 · 查明，确定
中文：v. 发现，查明，确定
单词：###ascertain###
搭配：###ascertain the facts### 查明事实
例句：Journalists must ###ascertain### the facts before publishing, since a story built on dubious, anecdotal sources is not credible.
译文：记者在发表之前必须查明事实，因为建立在可疑、道听途说的消息来源上的报道是不可信的。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0605 -->

中文：adj. 怀疑的，可疑的，令人怀疑的；靠不住的
单词：###dubious###
搭配：###a dubious claim### 可疑的说法
例句：Journalists must ascertain the facts before publishing, since a story built on ###dubious###, anecdotal sources is not credible.
译文：记者在发表之前必须查明事实，因为建立在可疑、道听途说的消息来源上的报道是不可信的。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1983 -->

搭配：###anecdotal evidence### 传闻证据
例句：Journalists must ascertain the facts before publishing, since a story built on dubious, ###anecdotal### sources is not credible.
译文：记者在发表之前必须查明事实，因为建立在可疑、道听途说的消息来源上的报道是不可信的。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0323 -->

搭配：###a credible witness### 可信的证人

<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1132 -->

中文：n. 派别，宗派，小集团；派系
单词：###faction###
搭配：###rival factions### 敌对派系
例句：Unless both ###factions### agree to disarm, the fragile peace may collapse and the conflict could escalate.
译文：除非双方派别都同意解除武装，否则脆弱的和平可能瓦解，冲突还可能升级。

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0804 -->

搭配：###edible mushrooms### 食用菌
例句：Many hikers dread eating wild mushrooms, so a botany expert should confirm which are ###edible###, since no one would deliberately risk poisoning.

例句：Many hikers ###dread### eating wild mushrooms, so a botany expert should confirm which are edible, since no one would deliberately risk poisoning.

单词：###microprocessor###
搭配：###a fast microprocessor### 快速的微处理器
例句：The professor drew an analogy between the intricate human brain and a ###microprocessor###, but the two are only partly analogous.
译文：教授把复杂的人脑比作微处理器，但两者只是部分相似。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0315 -->

<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0680 -->

搭配：###hum a tune### 哼曲子
例句：What's that ###humming### noise? — The radiator. — It's so irritating! — Turn the knob down a little.

# 六级单词 1056 · 隐约出现；逼近

译文：前方隐约出现冰山时，雾可能让船员看不清，但现代导航系统仍能探测到它。

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1566 -->
# 六级单词 1566 · 垄断；专营权

例句：Fancy a lottery ticket? — If we win, we'll buy a ###mansion### and live a lavish life! — Keep dreaming; the chance is nil.
译文：想买张彩票吗？——要是中了，我们就买豪宅，过奢华日子！——做梦吧，概率几乎为零。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

译文：你在抱怨什么？——那趟乏味的航班坐得我腿都麻了，旁边的人还一直嘟嘟囔囔。——真可怜！

搭配：###a sense of alienation### 疏离感
例句：Rapid urban growth can breed ###alienation### among migrants, and without assimilation their children may go astray, so cities should augment social services.
译文：城市的快速扩张可能让外来人口产生疏离感，如果不能融入，他们的孩子可能误入歧途，所以城市应该加强社会服务。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

搭配：###doze off### 打瞌睡
例句：How's your back? — Much better since I tried acupuncture. — Does it still ail you? — Only when I ###doze### in a cold train compartment.
译文：你的背怎么样了？——试了针灸以后好多了。——还会让你难受吗？——只有在冷冰冰的火车车厢里打盹时才疼。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0284 -->

搭配：###a train compartment### 火车车厢
例句：How's your back? — Much better since I tried acupuncture. — Does it still ail you? — Only when I doze in a cold train ###compartment###.

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0474 -->

例句：The ###capacitance### of a capacitor depends on two parameters: the area of its plates and the insulator placed between them.
译文：电容器的电容取决于两个参数：极板的面积和极板之间的绝缘体。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0478 -->

搭配：###a cardinal rule### 首要原则
例句：The ###cardinal### rule of journalism is that reporters must never fabricate quotations, invent eyewitness accounts or distort facts.
译文：新闻业的首要原则是，记者绝不能捏造引语、编造目击者证词或歪曲事实。

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0479 -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0152 -->

搭配：###juvenile crime### 青少年犯罪
例句：Experts argue that ###juvenile### offenders should be rehabilitated rather than imprisoned, since prison often perpetuates crime.
译文：专家认为，对少年犯应该帮助其改造，而不是关进监狱，因为监狱往往让犯罪延续下去。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w2072 -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w2073 -->

译文：快看！那只鸟嘴里叼着一块面包皮举得高高的。——它一直冲我们眨眼。——也许是在招呼我们分它点午饭。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w2080 -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w2083 -->
# 六级单词 2083 · 分配，配置
中文：n. 分配，安置；配置
单词：###allocation###
搭配：###resource allocation### 资源配置
例句：The ministry's ###allocation### of funds to athletics drew criticism, so auditors will appraise each project and publish their appraisal in an appendix.
译文：该部门给田径运动拨款的方式受到批评，所以审计人员将评估每个项目，并把评估结果附在附录里发布。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0170 -->

<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0502 -->
# 六级单词 0502 · 和弦
中文：n. 和弦，和音；(几何的)弦
单词：###chord###
搭配：###play a chord### 弹和弦
例句：Whenever I reach this ###chord###, I lose the tempo; could the drummer slow down so the melody doesn't fall apart?
译文：每次弹到这个和弦我就跟不上节奏，鼓手能慢一点吗？这样旋律就不会乱了。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0503 -->

搭配：###a fast tempo### 快节奏
例句：Whenever I reach this chord, I lose the ###tempo###; could the drummer slow down so the melody doesn't fall apart?
译文：每次弹到这个和弦我就跟不上节奏，鼓手能慢一点吗？这样旋律就不会乱了。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0504 -->

例句：Whenever I reach this chord, I lose the tempo; could the ###drummer### slow down so the melody doesn't fall apart?

<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0505 -->

搭配：###a beautiful melody### 优美的旋律

译文：每次弹到这个和弦我就跟不上节奏，鼓手能慢一点吗？这样旋律就不会乱了。

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w2085 -->
# 六级单词 2085 · 诱惑力，魅力；吸引

单词：###allure###
搭配：###the allure of### 的魅力
例句：The ###allure### of fame tempts many young people, yet few contemplate the bitterness that often ensues when the spotlight fades.
译文：名声的诱惑吸引着许多年轻人，但很少有人想过聚光灯熄灭后常常随之而来的苦涩。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0634 -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0981 -->
# 六级单词 0981 · 接着发生
中文：v. 接着发生，随之而来
单词：###ensue###
搭配：###in the ensuing years### 在随后几年里
例句：The allure of fame tempts many young people, yet few contemplate the bitterness that often ###ensues### when the spotlight fades.
译文：名声的诱惑吸引着许多年轻人，但很少有人想过聚光灯熄灭后常常随之而来的苦涩。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1163 -->
# 六级单词 1163 · 培养，养育
中文：v. 养育，教育；培育，扶植；培养
单词：###nurture###
搭配：###nurture talent### 培养人才
例句：Parents who ###nurture### their offspring's curiosity, never mocking naive questions, raise confident learners.
译文：那些呵护孩子好奇心、从不嘲笑天真问题的父母，能培养出自信的学习者。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0697 -->

中文：n. 子孙，后代；结果，产物；子女
单词：###offspring###
搭配：###their offspring### 他们的子女
例句：Parents who nurture their ###offspring###'s curiosity, never mocking naive questions, raise confident learners.
译文：那些呵护孩子好奇心、从不嘲笑天真问题的父母，能培养出自信的学习者。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1379 -->

搭配：###mock sb. for### 因……嘲笑某人
例句：Parents who nurture their offspring's curiosity, never ###mocking### naive questions, raise confident learners.
译文：那些呵护孩子好奇心、从不嘲笑天真问题的父母，能培养出自信的学习者。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1082 -->

例句：Parents who nurture their offspring's curiosity, never mocking ###naive### questions, raise confident learners.

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w2086 -->
# 六级单词 2086 · 贵族
中文：n. 贵族
单词：###aristocrat###
搭配：###a wealthy aristocrat### 富有的贵族
例句：In colonial times, a few ###aristocrats### amassed vast estates, while ordinary colonists endured harsh constraints on their liberty.
译文：殖民时期，少数贵族聚敛了大片庄园，而普通殖民者的自由却受到严厉限制。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0402 -->
# 六级单词 0402 · 气压计；晴雨表
中文：n. 气压计，晴雨表
单词：###barometer###
搭配：###a barometer of### 的晴雨表
例句：Did you check the ###barometer###? — It's falling fast; brace yourself for a storm. — Then let's move the cricket match to an alternate date.
译文：你看气压计了吗？——降得很快，准备好迎接暴风雨吧。——那我们把板球比赛改到别的日子吧。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0204 -->

单词：###brace###
搭配：###brace oneself for### 为……做好准备
例句：Did you check the barometer? — It's falling fast; ###brace### yourself for a storm. — Then let's move the cricket match to an alternate date.
译文：你看气压计了吗？——降得很快，准备好迎接暴风雨吧。——那我们把板球比赛改到别的日子吧。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

例句：Did you check the barometer? — It's falling fast; brace yourself for a storm. — Then let's move the ###cricket### match to an alternate date.
译文：你看气压计了吗？——降得很快，准备好迎接暴风雨吧。——那我们把板球比赛改到别的日子吧。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0298 -->
# 六级单词 0298 · 交替的；备选的；交替
中文：adj. 交替的，轮流的，交错的；备选的 v. (使)交替，(使)轮流
单词：###alternate###
搭配：###on alternate days### 隔天
例句：Did you check the barometer? — It's falling fast; brace yourself for a storm. — Then let's move the cricket match to an ###alternate### date.
译文：你看气压计了吗？——降得很快，准备好迎接暴风雨吧。——那我们把板球比赛改到别的日子吧。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w2089 -->
# 六级单词 2089 · 碳水化合物
中文：n. 碳水化合物；糖类；淀粉质或糖类
单词：###carbohydrate###
搭配：###low in carbohydrates### 低碳水化合物
例句：###Carbohydrates### fuel the brain, and whole grains complement lean protein, so a balanced meal can energize students and avert afternoon fatigue.
译文：碳水化合物为大脑供能，全谷物与瘦肉蛋白相互补充，所以均衡的饭菜能让学生精力充沛，避免下午犯困。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w2090 -->

搭配：###complement each other### 相辅相成
例句：Carbohydrates fuel the brain, and whole grains ###complement### lean protein, so a balanced meal can energize students and avert afternoon fatigue.
译文：碳水化合物为大脑供能，全谷物与瘦肉蛋白相互补充，所以均衡的饭菜能让学生精力充沛，避免下午犯困。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

搭配：###energize the team### 激发团队活力
例句：Carbohydrates fuel the brain, and whole grains complement lean protein, so a balanced meal can ###energize### students and avert afternoon fatigue.

<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w2091 -->
# 六级单词 2091 · 赚钱的，有利可图的
中文：adj. 赚钱的，获利的，有利可图的
单词：###lucrative###
搭配：###a lucrative business### 赚钱的生意
例句：So ###lucrative### are the oil fields that rival nations have long fought over maritime jurisdiction, a notoriously stubborn dispute.
译文：这些油田利润极其丰厚，以至于对立国家长期争夺海上管辖权，这是一场出了名难解决的纠纷。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

例句：Where's the ###arena###? — An array of flags marks the gate. — Is the band ready? — The bass player is tuning up; there's an assortment of snacks.

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0555 -->
# 六级单词 0555 · 审查；审查员

单词：###censor###
搭配：###censor the news### 审查新闻
例句：Authorities ###censor### critical posts and deport foreign journalists, yet dissidents continue to voice their dissent.

<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1632 -->
# 六级单词 1632 · 易于……的；俯卧的

例句：People ###prone### to an obsession with perfection may suffer paralysis when facing choices, and escaping this ordeal takes patience.
译文：过分追求完美的人在面临选择时可能会无所适从，要摆脱这种煎熬需要耐心。

搭配：###a train derailed### 火车出轨
例句：A freight train ###derailed### at a busy junction and nearly collided with a passenger train; rescue teams were dispatched at once.

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w2100 -->
# 六级单词 2100 · 虚张声势；吓唬；虚张声势
中文：n. 断崖，诈骗 adj. 绝壁的 v. 诈骗；虚张声势；吓唬
单词：###bluff###
搭配：###call sb.'s bluff### 揭穿某人
例句：Why was the burglar caught so fast? — He tried to ###bluff### the guard, but he blushed and stammered. — And the police bust took only minutes.
译文：那个窃贼怎么这么快就被抓了？——他想骗过保安，结果脸红结巴了。——警察突击抓捕只用了几分钟。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0550 -->

译文：连着三个晚上牙疼得要命，我去看了牙医，他发现了一个很深的蛀洞，把那颗牙拔了。

搭配：###correlate with### 与……相关
例句：The two variables ###correlate### with each other, but the coefficient in the composite index is small, so any deduction about causes would be premature.
译文：这两个变量相关，但在综合指数中的系数很小，所以对原因下任何推论都为时过早。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0567 -->

例句：The two variables correlate with each other, but the ###coefficient### in the composite index is small, so any deduction about causes would be premature.
译文：这两个变量相关，但在综合指数中的系数很小，所以对原因下任何推论都为时过早。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w2117 -->

单词：###clench###
搭配：###clench one's teeth### 咬紧牙关
例句：Why are you ###clenching### your fists? — The music next door could deafen anyone! — Start with a compliment on his taste, and don't be confrontational.
译文：你攥着拳头干什么？——隔壁的音乐能把人震聋！——先夸夸他的品位，别跟他对着干。

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0658 -->
# 六级单词 0658 · 软木塞；用塞子塞住

搭配：###pop the cork### 拔开瓶塞
例句：Can you pull out the ###cork###? — It's crumbling; I'll use the cutter on my knife. — Or should we brew tea instead? — No, tonight's a feast!
译文：你能把瓶塞拔出来吗？——它碎了，我用小刀上的切割器试试。——要不我们还是泡茶吧？——不，今晚是大餐！
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w2119 -->

搭配：###affirm one's support### 表明支持

译文：尽管委员会重申了对公平的承诺，却只把一小部分资金分给了城市周边的贫困地区。

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w2122 -->
# 六级单词 2122 · 天然的，未加工的；粗鲁的

单词：###crude###
搭配：###crude oil### 原油
例句：When ###crude### oil prices fall, the currencies of exporting nations depreciate, and governments must divert funds to avoid a default on their debts.
译文：原油价格下跌时，出口国的货币会贬值，政府必须调拨资金，以免债务违约。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w2123 -->

例句：Who's the girl with ###curly### hair? — My cousin. — She's conspicuous in that red dress, and her complexion is lovely! — She's also a dutiful daughter.
译文：那个卷发女孩是谁？——我表妹。——她穿那条红裙子特别显眼，气色也好！——她还是个很孝顺的女儿。

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w2128 -->

中文：n. 避难，庇护；避难所
单词：###refuge###
搭配：###take refuge in### 在……避难
例句：The village became a ###refuge### for a multitude of flood victims, and volunteers handed out rations so that nobody would perish.

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0794 -->
# 六级单词 0794 · 冷漠的；独立的

单词：###detached###
搭配：###a detached house### 独栋房屋
例句：Your son seems ###detached### lately. — We emigrated last month and he misses his friends. — Be discreet; a hug may be the best consolation.
译文：你儿子最近好像有点疏离。——我们上个月移民了，他想念朋友。——别太刻意，一个拥抱也许就是最好的安慰。

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0775 -->

<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w2131 -->
# 六级单词 2131 · 空气传播的；空运的

单词：###airborne###
搭配：###airborne viruses### 空气传播的病毒
例句：Doctors say ###airborne### pollutants can be akin to tobacco smoke, damaging the arteries, so cities must amplify efforts to clean the air.
译文：医生说，空气中的污染物危害可能与烟草烟雾相似，会损伤动脉，所以城市必须加大治理空气的力度。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w2132 -->

单词：###akin###
搭配：###akin to### 类似于
例句：Doctors say airborne pollutants can be ###akin### to tobacco smoke, damaging the arteries, so cities must amplify efforts to clean the air.
译文：医生说，空气中的污染物危害可能与烟草烟雾相似，会损伤动脉，所以城市必须加大治理空气的力度。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0183 -->
# 六级单词 0183 · 动脉；干线
中文：n. 动脉；干线，要道(道路、铁路等)
单词：###artery###
搭配：###a main artery### 交通干线
例句：Doctors say airborne pollutants can be akin to tobacco smoke, damaging the ###arteries###, so cities must amplify efforts to clean the air.
译文：医生说，空气中的污染物危害可能与烟草烟雾相似，会损伤动脉，所以城市必须加大治理空气的力度。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w2133 -->
# 六级单词 2133 · 放大；增强
中文：v. 详述，详加解说；放大(声音等)，增强(音量)
单词：###amplify###
搭配：###amplify sound### 放大声音
例句：Doctors say airborne pollutants can be akin to tobacco smoke, damaging the arteries, so cities must ###amplify### efforts to clean the air.
译文：医生说，空气中的污染物危害可能与烟草烟雾相似，会损伤动脉，所以城市必须加大治理空气的力度。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w2134 -->

单词：###emulate###
搭配：###emulate one's heroes### 效仿偶像
例句：Teenagers often ###emulate### celebrities and repeat clichés, so teachers should elicit their own views and endow them with critical thinking.
译文：青少年常常模仿名人、重复陈词滥调，所以老师应该引导他们说出自己的看法，培养他们的批判性思维。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

例句：Teenagers often emulate celebrities and repeat ###clichés###, so teachers should elicit their own views and endow them with critical thinking.

<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w2136 -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w2139 -->

中文：n. 反义词

搭配：###antonyms and synonyms### 反义词和同义词
例句：To use words correctly, learners should know that the ###antonym### of "fearless" is "timid" and that "slim" usually has a positive connotation.
译文：要想正确用词，学习者应该知道 fearless 的反义词是 timid，而 slim 通常带褒义。

例句：To use words correctly, learners should know that the antonym of "###fearless###" is "timid" and that "slim" usually has a positive connotation.

例句：To use words ###correctly###, learners should know that the antonym of "fearless" is "timid" and that "slim" usually has a positive connotation.

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w2140 -->

单词：###assault###
搭配：###sexual assault### 性侵犯
例句：Why was that player sent off? — He ###assaulted### an opponent. — What a disgrace; it appals me. — His ejection was fully justified.
译文：那个球员为什么被罚下？——他袭击了对手。——太丢人了，我都震惊了。——把他罚下场完全合理。

单词：###disgrace###
搭配：###in disgrace### 丢脸地
例句：Why was that player sent off? — He assaulted an opponent. — What a ###disgrace###; it appals me. — His ejection was fully justified.
译文：那个球员为什么被罚下？——他袭击了对手。——太丢人了，我都震惊了。——把他罚下场完全合理。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w2148 -->

单词：###appal###
搭配：###be appalled by### 对……感到震惊

译文：那个球员为什么被罚下？——他袭击了对手。——太丢人了，我都震惊了。——把他罚下场完全合理。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w2149 -->

搭配：###ejection from the game### 被逐出比赛

<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0201 -->
# 六级单词 0201 · 使困惑
中文：v. 使迷惑，困惑
单词：###baffle###
搭配：###be baffled by### 被……难住
例句：The film ###baffled### critics: the director contrived a twist that culminates in chaos, although its visual esthetic is gorgeous.
译文：这部电影让影评人一头雾水：导演硬编了一个反转，结局一团乱，不过画面美学非常漂亮。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0651 -->

单词：###contrive###
搭配：###contrive to do### 设法做
例句：The film baffled critics: the director ###contrived### a twist that culminates in chaos, although its visual esthetic is gorgeous.
译文：这部电影让影评人一头雾水：导演硬编了一个反转，结局一团乱，不过画面美学非常漂亮。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1044 -->

搭配：###culminate in### 以……告终
例句：The film baffled critics: the director contrived a twist that ###culminates### in chaos, although its visual esthetic is gorgeous.
译文：这部电影让影评人一头雾水：导演硬编了一个反转，结局一团乱，不过画面美学非常漂亮。

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1107 -->

中文：adj. 审美的，感觉的；美学的
单词：###esthetic###
搭配：###an esthetic sense### 审美意识
例句：The film baffled critics: the director contrived a twist that culminates in chaos, although its visual ###esthetic### is gorgeous.
译文：这部电影让影评人一头雾水：导演硬编了一个反转，结局一团乱，不过画面美学非常漂亮。

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0358 -->
# 六级单词 0358 · 把……归因于
中文：v. 把…归因于，认为…是某人所做
单词：###ascribe###
搭配：###ascribe A to B### 把 A 归因于 B
例句：Historians ###ascribe### the assassination of the king to a cult whose members had conspired for years.
译文：历史学家把国王遇刺归因于一个邪教团体，其成员已密谋多年。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0369 -->

中文：n. 暗杀，行刺
单词：###assassination###
搭配：###an assassination attempt### 暗杀企图
例句：Historians ascribe the ###assassination### of the king to a cult whose members had conspired for years.
译文：历史学家把国王遇刺归因于一个邪教团体，其成员已密谋多年。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w2150 -->

例句：Historians ascribe the assassination of the king to a ###cult### whose members had conspired for years.

<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w2151 -->

中文：v. 密谋，共谋
单词：###conspire###
搭配：###conspire against### 密谋反对
例句：Historians ascribe the assassination of the king to a cult whose members had ###conspired### for years.

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0654 -->

搭配：###a user interface### 用户界面

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0830 -->
# 六级单词 0830 · 断开
中文：v. 断开
单词：###disconnect###
搭配：###disconnect the power### 切断电源
例句：Can you ###disconnect### the gas burner? The flame is abnormally high. — Sure; let's also avail ourselves of the fresh air and open the windows.
译文：你能把燃气灶关掉吗？火苗高得不正常。——好的，我们也开窗透透新鲜空气吧。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0454 -->
# 六级单词 0454 · 炉头；燃烧器

单词：###burner###
搭配：###a gas burner### 燃气灶
例句：Can you disconnect the gas ###burner###? The flame is abnormally high. — Sure; let's also avail ourselves of the fresh air and open the windows.
译文：你能把燃气灶关掉吗？火苗高得不正常。——好的，我们也开窗透透新鲜空气吧。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

搭配：###abnormally high### 异常高
例句：Can you disconnect the gas burner? The flame is ###abnormally### high. — Sure; let's also avail ourselves of the fresh air and open the windows.

搭配：###avail oneself of### 利用；to no avail 徒劳

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0262 -->
# 六级单词 0262 · 宗族，部族
中文：n. 氏族，家族，宗派；宗族；部族
单词：###clan###
搭配：###a Scottish clan### 苏格兰氏族
例句：Rival ###clans### once lived in constant discord until they formed a confederation and conferred equal rights on every member.
译文：敌对的部族曾经长期不和，直到他们组成联盟，赋予每个成员平等的权利。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w2153 -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w2154 -->

单词：###confederation###
搭配：###a loose confederation### 松散的联盟
例句：Rival clans once lived in constant discord until they formed a ###confederation### and conferred equal rights on every member.

搭配：###confer a degree on### 授予……学位
例句：Rival clans once lived in constant discord until they formed a confederation and ###conferred### equal rights on every member.

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0160 -->
# 六级单词 0160 · 小册子
中文：n. 小册子
单词：###booklet###
搭配：###an information booklet### 资料手册
例句：Who wrote this ###booklet###? — Leiji, a teacher from Cairnote. — Her anecdotes are so apt! — Yes, she's ardent about words.
译文：这本小册子是谁写的？——磊记，一位来自 Cairnote 的老师。——她讲的小故事真贴切！——是啊，她对词汇特别有热情。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w2155 -->

<!-- /leiji-note -->

搭配：###an ardent supporter### 热心的支持者
例句：Who wrote this booklet? — Leiji, a teacher from Cairnote. — Her anecdotes are so apt! — Yes, she's ###ardent### about words.

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w2157 -->
# 六级单词 2157 · 清洗；净化
中文：v. 清洗；净化
单词：###cleanse###
搭配：###cleanse the skin### 清洁皮肤
例句：Nurses ###cleanse### each wound and dispense medicine, yet staff shortages lead to dissatisfaction and a degeneration of care.
译文：护士们清洗每处伤口、分发药品，但人手短缺导致不满情绪，护理质量也在下降。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w2158 -->

搭配：###dispense with### 免除
例句：Nurses cleanse each wound and ###dispense### medicine, yet staff shortages lead to dissatisfaction and a degeneration of care.
译文：护士们清洗每处伤口、分发药品，但人手短缺导致不满情绪，护理质量也在下降。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0003 -->

单词：###dissatisfaction###
搭配：###express dissatisfaction### 表示不满
例句：Nurses cleanse each wound and dispense medicine, yet staff shortages lead to ###dissatisfaction### and a degeneration of care.
译文：护士们清洗每处伤口、分发药品，但人手短缺导致不满情绪，护理质量也在下降。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0740 -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w2159 -->
# 六级单词 2159 · 缓冲物；缓冲
中文：n. 缓冲器；缓冲物
单词：###buffer###
搭配：###act as a buffer### 起缓冲作用
例句：Were it not for the ###buffer### of public grants, small theatres would forfeit their autonomy and depend on a single sponsor's endorsement.
译文：要不是有公共拨款作缓冲，小剧场就会失去自主权，只能依赖某一家赞助商的支持。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w2160 -->

搭配：###forfeit the right### 丧失权利
例句：Were it not for the buffer of public grants, small theatres would ###forfeit### their autonomy and depend on a single sponsor's endorsement.
译文：要不是有公共拨款作缓冲，小剧场就会失去自主权，只能依赖某一家赞助商的支持。

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0137 -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0967 -->

搭配：###a celebrity endorsement### 名人代言
例句：Were it not for the buffer of public grants, small theatres would forfeit their autonomy and depend on a single sponsor's ###endorsement###.

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0667 -->
# 六级单词 0667 · 到期；去世
中文：v. 期满，终止；死亡；呼出，吐气；到期；去世
单词：###expire###
搭配：###expire at midnight### 午夜到期
例句：My passport will ###expire### soon. — Renew it; the fee isn't extraordinarily high. — Is the express service dispensable? — Yes; normal service takes a fortnight.
译文：我的护照快到期了。——去续吧，费用并不特别高。——加急服务可以不要吗？——可以，普通的只要两周。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1123 -->

例句：My passport will expire soon. — Renew it; the fee isn't ###extraordinarily### high. — Is the express service dispensable? — Yes; normal service takes a fortnight.
译文：我的护照快到期了。——去续吧，费用并不特别高。——加急服务可以不要吗？——可以，普通的只要两周。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0760 -->

<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w2161 -->

搭配：###a fortnight ago### 两周前

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w2162 -->

中文：adj. 一致的；同时的；同时发生的
单词：###concurrent###
搭配：###concurrent events### 同时发生的事件
例句：The museum runs two ###concurrent### expositions: one displays a corpse preserved in ice, and the other traces the extraction of minerals.
译文：博物馆同时举办两场展览：一场展出一具冰封的遗体，另一场追溯矿物开采的历史。

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0896 -->

例句：The museum runs two concurrent expositions: one displays a ###corpse### preserved in ice, and the other traces the extraction of minerals.

<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1119 -->

单词：###extraction###
搭配：###oil extraction### 石油开采

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w2163 -->
# 六级单词 2163 · 佳肴；精致；微妙

单词：###delicacy###
搭配：###a local delicacy### 当地美食
例句：What's this ###delicacy###? — Fried insects, with a sauce the chef distilled from herbs. — I'll pass. — Don't despise it; just dilute the sauce with water.
译文：这道佳肴是什么？——炸昆虫，配主厨用香草提炼的酱汁。——我就不吃了。——先别嫌弃，酱汁兑点水就行。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1088 -->

搭配：###distilled water### 蒸馏水
例句：What's this delicacy? — Fried insects, with a sauce the chef ###distilled### from herbs. — I'll pass. — Don't despise it; just dilute the sauce with water.
译文：这道佳肴是什么？——炸昆虫，配主厨用香草提炼的酱汁。——我就不吃了。——先别嫌弃，酱汁兑点水就行。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w2164 -->

搭配：###despise sb. for### 因……鄙视某人
例句：What's this delicacy? — Fried insects, with a sauce the chef distilled from herbs. — I'll pass. — Don't ###despise### it; just dilute the sauce with water.
译文：这道佳肴是什么？——炸昆虫，配主厨用香草提炼的酱汁。——我就不吃了。——先别嫌弃，酱汁兑点水就行。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0780 -->

例句：What's this delicacy? — Fried insects, with a sauce the chef distilled from herbs. — I'll pass. — Don't despise it; just ###dilute### the sauce with water.
译文：这道佳肴是什么？——炸昆虫，配主厨用香草提炼的酱汁。——我就不吃了。——先别嫌弃，酱汁兑点水就行。

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w2165 -->

中文：n. 下士 adj. 肉体的
单词：###corporal###
搭配：###corporal punishment### 体罚
例句：The novel portrays a ###corporal### crippled in battle who conceives a plan to emancipate the prisoners.
译文：这部小说描写了一位在战斗中致残的下士，他想出了一个解救囚犯的计划。

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w2166 -->

中文：n. 跛足的人，残废人；跛子 v. 使…残废；使…受损害，使…丧失活动能力；使陷入瘫痪

搭配：###be crippled by debt### 被债务拖垮
例句：The novel portrays a corporal ###crippled### in battle who conceives a plan to emancipate the prisoners.
译文：这部小说描写了一位在战斗中致残的下士，他想出了一个解救囚犯的计划。

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0606 -->

搭配：###conceive of### 设想
例句：The novel portrays a corporal crippled in battle who ###conceives### a plan to emancipate the prisoners.
译文：这部小说描写了一位在战斗中致残的下士，他想出了一个解救囚犯的计划。

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w2167 -->

<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w2168 -->
# 六级单词 2168 · 监护；拘留
中文：n. 监护；拘留
单词：###custody###
搭配：###in custody### 被拘留
例句：Courts may grant ###custody### to either parent, but judges weigh the child's disposition and the detriment a move would entail.
译文：法院可能把抚养权判给父母任何一方，但法官会权衡孩子的性情以及搬家可能带来的不利影响。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1079 -->
# 六级单词 1079 · 性情；处置
中文：n. 性格，性情；处置
单词：###disposition###
搭配：###a cheerful disposition### 开朗的性情
例句：Courts may grant custody to either parent, but judges weigh the child's ###disposition### and the detriment a move would entail.
译文：法院可能把抚养权判给父母任何一方，但法官会权衡孩子的性情以及搬家可能带来的不利影响。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w2169 -->

搭配：###to the detriment of### 有损于
例句：Courts may grant custody to either parent, but judges weigh the child's disposition and the ###detriment### a move would entail.
译文：法院可能把抚养权判给父母任何一方，但法官会权衡孩子的性情以及搬家可能带来的不利影响。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0637 -->

单词：###entail###
搭配：###entail risks### 带来风险
例句：Courts may grant custody to either parent, but judges weigh the child's disposition and the detriment a move would ###entail###.
译文：法院可能把抚养权判给父母任何一方，但法官会权衡孩子的性情以及搬家可能带来的不利影响。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w2170 -->
# 六级单词 2170 · 室，腔；会议厅

单词：###chamber###
搭配：###a gas chamber### 毒气室
例句：The engineers tested six ###chambers###, each fitted with a valve of light alloy, to see whether compression would deform or corrode them.
译文：工程师测试了六个腔室，每个都装有轻合金阀门，看压缩是否会使它们变形或腐蚀。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w2172 -->
# 六级单词 2172 · 中止；流产
中文：v. 夭折，中止；使流产
单词：###abort###
搭配：###abort a mission### 中止任务
例句：Did the pilot ###abort### the landing? — Yes, strong wind hit the glider and the crowd went frantic. — Was anyone hurt? — No, he wore a helmet.
译文：飞行员中止着陆了吗？——是的，一阵大风刮到了滑翔机，人群都慌了。——有人受伤吗？——没有，他戴着头盔。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0882 -->

搭配：###fly a glider### 驾驶滑翔机
例句：Did the pilot abort the landing? — Yes, strong wind hit the ###glider### and the crowd went frantic. — Was anyone hurt? — No, he wore a helmet.

搭配：###frantic efforts### 拼命的努力
例句：Did the pilot abort the landing? — Yes, strong wind hit the glider and the crowd went ###frantic###. — Was anyone hurt? — No, he wore a helmet.
译文：飞行员中止着陆了吗？——是的，一阵大风刮到了滑翔机，人群都慌了。——有人受伤吗？——没有，他戴着头盔。

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w2174 -->
# 六级单词 2174 · 解除管制
中文：v. 解除管制
单词：###deregulate###
搭配：###deregulate the industry### 放松行业管制
例句：When governments ###deregulate### markets too fast, distortion of prices may follow, and giant firms can dwarf small rivals, leaving little room for differentiation.
译文：政府过快放松市场管制时，价格可能扭曲，巨头企业会让小对手相形见绌，几乎没有差异化的空间。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1096 -->

搭配：###a distortion of facts### 歪曲事实
例句：When governments deregulate markets too fast, ###distortion### of prices may follow, and giant firms can dwarf small rivals, leaving little room for differentiation.
译文：政府过快放松市场管制时，价格可能扭曲，巨头企业会让小对手相形见绌，几乎没有差异化的空间。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0907 -->

单词：###dwarf###
搭配：###be dwarfed by### 被……比下去
例句：When governments deregulate markets too fast, distortion of prices may follow, and giant firms can ###dwarf### small rivals, leaving little room for differentiation.
译文：政府过快放松市场管制时，价格可能扭曲，巨头企业会让小对手相形见绌，几乎没有差异化的空间。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0810 -->

搭配：###product differentiation### 产品差异化

<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0279 -->
# 六级单词 0279 · 隶属关系；从属

单词：###affiliation###
搭配：###political affiliation### 政治派别
例句：Does your club have any ###affiliation### with the union? — No, but we affix its logo to our leaflets. — Isn't that illicit?
译文：你们俱乐部和工会有隶属关系吗？——没有，不过我们在传单上贴了工会的标志。——那不是违规的吗？
<!-- /leiji-note -->

例句：Does your club have any affiliation with the union? — No, but we affix its logo to our ###leaflets###. — Isn't that illicit?

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0373 -->
# 六级单词 0373 · 断言；维护
中文：v. 宣称，断言；维护，坚持(权利等)
单词：###assert###
搭配：###assert one's rights### 维护权利
例句：Opponents ###assert### that plans to denationalize the railways may spark antagonism among workers, while supporters equate private ownership with efficiency.
译文：反对者声称，铁路私有化计划可能激起工人的对立情绪，而支持者则把私有制等同于效率。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0758 -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w2177 -->

搭配：###antagonism between### 之间的对立

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w2179 -->

例句：Why is Dad so ###agitated###? — He's agonizing over the invoice. — Is it that high? — It's agony just to read it!
译文：爸爸为什么这么焦躁？——他在为那张发票发愁。——有那么贵吗？——光看一眼都让人痛苦！

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0143 -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0956 -->

搭配：###be embedded in### 嵌在……中

<!-- /leiji-note -->

例句：The eclipse coincided with the city's festival: the brightness faded and darkness ###enveloped### the town as the moon moved in an arc.

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0348 -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0571 -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w2183 -->

搭配：###deduce from### 从……推断
例句：It was an excerpt from an ancient chronicle, together with several artifacts found in the cemetery, that enabled scholars to ###deduce### the town's age.
译文：正是古代编年史中的一段摘录，加上墓地里发现的几件文物，让学者们推断出了这座小镇的年代。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w2186 -->

搭配：###allude to### 暗指
例句：Are you ###alluding### to me? — No! I meant the idiot who stole my parking slot. — Calm down; don't be so irritable.
译文：你是在影射我吗？——不是！我说的是那个抢了我车位的蠢货。——冷静点，别这么暴躁。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0557 -->

例句：Her ###discourse### on ethics, rich in elaboration, showed how ego can delude leaders into believing they are faultless.

搭配：###decode a message### 破译信息

<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w2212 -->

单词：###benevolent###
搭配：###a benevolent fund### 慈善基金
例句：A ###benevolent### foundation helps students beset by poverty, but each grant is contingent on grades, and recipients must abide by strict rules.
译文：一家慈善基金会资助深陷贫困的学生，但每笔资助都取决于成绩，受助者还必须遵守严格的规定。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- /leiji-note -->

搭配：###abide by### 遵守

搭配：###an elastic band### 橡皮筋
例句：Is this rope ###elastic###? — No, but it's sturdy. — Can it hold a swing? — Sure; tie it tightly to that oak.
译文：这根绳子有弹性吗？——没有，但很结实。——能挂秋千吗？——当然，把它紧紧系在那棵橡树上。

搭配：###a narrow inlet### 狭窄的水湾

译文：这个帝国吞并了半岛，并在山脊上驻扎了一支民兵来守卫海湾入口。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0459 -->
# 六级单词 0459 · 可卡因

单词：###cocaine###
搭配：###cocaine trafficking### 可卡因走私
例句：Customs officers seized ###cocaine### hidden in a cluster of crates, and police refused to disclose how a convict on parole had smuggled it.
译文：海关官员查获了藏在一堆板条箱里的可卡因，警方拒绝透露一名假释犯是如何把它走私进来的。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

单词：###flirt###
搭配：###flirt with danger### 玩火
例句：Is he ###flirting### with you? — Maybe; he keeps winking. — Look, your face is flushed! — Stop teasing me!
译文：他是在跟你调情吗？——也许吧，他一直冲我眨眼。——看，你脸都红了！——别取笑我了！

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0747 -->
# 六级单词 0747 · 退化；降级；堕落
中文：n. 降级；退化；衰变；堕落
单词：###degradation###
搭配：###environmental degradation### 环境退化
例句：###Degradation### and pollution have spoiled the marsh where geese once congregated, and only a fringe of grass endures, a feeble reminder of its past.
译文：生态退化和污染毁了这片大雁曾经聚集的沼泽，只剩边缘一圈野草，微弱地提醒着人们它昔日的样子。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

搭配：###a bone fracture### 骨折
例句：How did you get that ###fracture###? — I tumbled down a ski slope. — Did you hurt your thigh too? — Just bruised; I groaned all night.
译文：你怎么骨折的？——我从滑雪坡上摔了下来。——大腿也伤了吗？——只是淤青，我疼得哼了一整晚。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

中文：adv. 以前，从前
单词：###formerly###
搭配：###formerly known as### 以前称为
例句：Investors cheered when the firm, ###formerly### a loss-maker, raised its dividend, a sign that its endeavor to expand was economically sound.

例句：Agencies may ###downgrade### a country whose currency is volatile, prompting investors to reshuffle their portfolios.

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0403 -->
# 六级单词 0403 · 预示；宣布；先驱
中文：n. 传令官；通报者；先驱 v. 预示；宣布
单词：###herald###
搭配：###herald a new era### 预示新时代
例句：Got your ticket? — Yes, for the gospel concert by the windmill. — It ###heralds### the holiday season. — I'll relish every song!
译文：票拿到了吗？——拿到了，是风车旁边那场福音音乐会。——这预示着假期季要开始了。——我要好好享受每一首歌！
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1359 -->
# 六级单词 1359 · 享受；期待；乐趣
中文：n. 风味，美味，开胃小菜，佐餐品；喜好，兴趣；乐趣 v. 享受；期待
单词：###relish###
搭配：###relish the challenge### 乐于接受挑战
例句：Got your ticket? — Yes, for the gospel concert by the windmill. — It heralds the holiday season. — I'll ###relish### every song!
译文：票拿到了吗？——拿到了，是风车旁边那场福音音乐会。——这预示着假期季要开始了。——我要好好享受每一首歌！
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0802 -->
# 六级单词 0802 · 偏离
中文：v. 背离，偏离，越(轨)
单词：###deviate###
搭配：###deviate from the plan### 偏离计划
例句：The ship ###deviated### from its route to avoid a storm, and during the long deviation many seasick passengers caused turmoil on deck.
译文：船为了躲避风暴偏离了航线，在漫长的绕行中，许多晕船的乘客让甲板上一片混乱。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0807 -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w2254 -->
# 六级单词 2254 · 用力举起；举起
中文：v. 举起，拉，拖；投掷；(有节奏地)起伏；用力举起
单词：###heave###
搭配：###heave a sigh### 叹一口气
例句：Can you help me ###heave### this box? — Sure, what's in it? — Linen. — OK, don't stoop; bend your knees, and we'll hoist it onto the shelf.
译文：你能帮我抬一下这个箱子吗？——行，里面是什么？——亚麻布。——好，别弯腰，屈膝，我们把它举到架子上去。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w2255 -->

<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1063 -->
# 六级单词 1063 · 脱离；撤离
中文：n. 分离；脱离；撤离
单词：###disengagement###
搭配：###military disengagement### 军事脱离接触
例句：###Disengagement### from the conflict will require multilateral talks rather than unilateral action, and both sides must uphold the agreement.
译文：要摆脱冲突，需要多边谈判而不是单方面行动，双方都必须维护协议。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w2257 -->

中文：adj. 多边的
单词：###multilateral###
搭配：###multilateral trade### 多边贸易
例句：Disengagement from the conflict will require ###multilateral### talks rather than unilateral action, and both sides must uphold the agreement.
译文：要摆脱冲突，需要多边谈判而不是单方面行动，双方都必须维护协议。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w2258 -->
# 六级单词 2258 · 单方面的
中文：adj. 单方面的
单词：###unilateral###
搭配：###a unilateral decision### 单方面决定
例句：Disengagement from the conflict will require multilateral talks rather than ###unilateral### action, and both sides must uphold the agreement.
译文：要摆脱冲突，需要多边谈判而不是单方面行动，双方都必须维护协议。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1734 -->

单词：###uphold###
搭配：###uphold the law### 维护法律
例句：Disengagement from the conflict will require multilateral talks rather than unilateral action, and both sides must ###uphold### the agreement.

<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0799 -->
# 六级单词 0799 · 含铁的
中文：adj. 铁的；亚铁的；含铁的
单词：###ferrous###
搭配：###ferrous metals### 黑色金属
例句：###Ferrous### metals rust in humid air, so their surfaces are coated to keep them from oxidizing.
译文：含铁金属在潮湿空气中会生锈，所以表面要涂一层保护层，防止氧化。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w2259 -->

单词：###rust###
搭配：###rust away### 锈蚀
例句：Ferrous metals ###rust### in humid air, so their surfaces are coated to keep them from oxidizing.
译文：含铁金属在潮湿空气中会生锈，所以表面要涂一层保护层，防止氧化。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w2260 -->

中文：adj. 湿的，湿气重的；潮湿的
单词：###humid###
搭配：###hot and humid### 湿热的
例句：Ferrous metals rust in ###humid### air, so their surfaces are coated to keep them from oxidizing.
译文：含铁金属在潮湿空气中会生锈，所以表面要涂一层保护层，防止氧化。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0326 -->

中文：v. 氧化，使生锈
单词：###oxidize###
搭配：###be easily oxidized### 容易被氧化
例句：Ferrous metals rust in humid air, so their surfaces are coated to keep them from ###oxidizing###.
译文：含铁金属在潮湿空气中会生锈，所以表面要涂一层保护层，防止氧化。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0952 -->
# 六级单词 0952 · 流利地
中文：adv. 流利地
单词：###fluently###
搭配：###speak fluently### 说得流利
例句：You speak French ###fluently###! — I immersed myself in it for a year. — Any tips? — Mistakes inevitably happen; don't let them humiliate you.
译文：你法语说得真流利！——我在法语环境里泡了一年。——有什么诀窍吗？——犯错在所难免，别因此觉得丢脸。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0939 -->

搭配：###be immersed in### 沉浸于
例句：You speak French fluently! — I ###immersed### myself in it for a year. — Any tips? — Mistakes inevitably happen; don't let them humiliate you.
译文：你法语说得真流利！——我在法语环境里泡了一年。——有什么诀窍吗？——犯错在所难免，别因此觉得丢脸。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0873 -->

中文：adv. 不可避免地

搭配：###inevitably lead to### 必然导致
例句：You speak French fluently! — I immersed myself in it for a year. — Any tips? — Mistakes ###inevitably### happen; don't let them humiliate you.
译文：你法语说得真流利！——我在法语环境里泡了一年。——有什么诀窍吗？——犯错在所难免，别因此觉得丢脸。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w2261 -->

中文：v. 使羞辱，使丢脸
单词：###humiliate###
搭配：###feel humiliated### 感到屈辱
例句：You speak French fluently! — I immersed myself in it for a year. — Any tips? — Mistakes inevitably happen; don't let them ###humiliate### you.
译文：你法语说得真流利！——我在法语环境里泡了一年。——有什么诀窍吗？——犯错在所难免，别因此觉得丢脸。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1099 -->
# 六级单词 1099 · 失业救济金；发放
中文：n. 救济，救济金；失业救济金 vt. 发放，发给
单词：###dole###
搭配：###on the dole### 领失业救济
例句：Made redundant at the mine where he had toiled for years, my brother is now on the ###dole###, hoping to regain his confidence.
译文：我哥哥在干了多年的矿上被裁员，现在靠失业救济金过活，希望能重拾信心。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1782 -->

单词：###redundant###
搭配：###be made redundant### 被裁员
例句：Made ###redundant### at the mine where he had toiled for years, my brother is now on the dole, hoping to regain his confidence.
译文：我哥哥在干了多年的矿上被裁员，现在靠失业救济金过活，希望能重拾信心。

单词：###toil###
搭配：###toil away### 苦干
例句：Made redundant at the mine where he had ###toiled### for years, my brother is now on the dole, hoping to regain his confidence.
译文：我哥哥在干了多年的矿上被裁员，现在靠失业救济金过活，希望能重拾信心。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1343 -->
# 六级单词 1343 · 重新获得；恢复
中文：v. 重新获得；恢复
单词：###regain###
搭配：###regain consciousness### 恢复知觉
例句：Made redundant at the mine where he had toiled for years, my brother is now on the dole, hoping to ###regain### his confidence.
译文：我哥哥在干了多年的矿上被裁员，现在靠失业救济金过活，希望能重拾信心。

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1180 -->
# 六级单词 1180 · 使变平；击败
中文：v. 把…弄平；击倒；使变平；击败
单词：###flatten###
搭配：###flatten the curve### 使曲线变平
例句：Workers ###flattened### the slum overnight, and the outrage that followed forced the council to reclaim land for its tenants.
译文：工人们一夜之间铲平了贫民窟，随之而来的公愤迫使市政会为租户收回土地。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w2263 -->

中文：n. 贫民区，贫民窟
单词：###slum###
搭配：###live in a slum### 住在贫民窟
例句：Workers flattened the ###slum### overnight, and the outrage that followed forced the council to reclaim land for its tenants.

<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0243 -->

中文：n. 暴行，残暴；(暴行激起的)义愤；愤慨 v. 激起…的义愤；违背；激怒
单词：###outrage###
搭配：###public outrage### 公愤
例句：Workers flattened the slum overnight, and the ###outrage### that followed forced the council to reclaim land for its tenants.
译文：工人们一夜之间铲平了贫民窟，随之而来的公愤迫使市政会为租户收回土地。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1122 -->

中文：v. 要回，回收；开垦(荒地)；改造，感化(罪犯等)
单词：###reclaim###
搭配：###reclaim land### 开垦土地
例句：Workers flattened the slum overnight, and the outrage that followed forced the council to ###reclaim### land for its tenants.
译文：工人们一夜之间铲平了贫民窟，随之而来的公愤迫使市政会为租户收回土地。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w2264 -->
# 六级单词 2264 · 租户
中文：n. 房客，租户；佃户
单词：###tenant###
搭配：###a tenant farmer### 佃农
例句：Workers flattened the slum overnight, and the outrage that followed forced the council to reclaim land for its ###tenants###.
译文：工人们一夜之间铲平了贫民窟，随之而来的公愤迫使市政会为租户收回土地。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0898 -->
# 六级单词 0898 · 配音；给……起绰号
中文：v. 起绰号；(为影片)配音
单词：###dub###
搭配：###be dubbed into Chinese### 被译制成中文
例句：When a film is ###dubbed###, the hero's sarcasm, vocal tone and subtle humour are often lost, so many viewers prefer the original.
译文：电影一旦配音，主角的挖苦、声音语气和微妙的幽默往往就没了，所以很多观众更喜欢原版。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w2265 -->
# 六级单词 2265 · 讽刺，挖苦
中文：n. 讽刺，挖苦
单词：###sarcasm###
搭配：###heavy sarcasm### 辛辣的讽刺
例句：When a film is dubbed, the hero's ###sarcasm###, vocal tone and subtle humour are often lost, so many viewers prefer the original.
译文：电影一旦配音，主角的挖苦、声音语气和微妙的幽默往往就没了，所以很多观众更喜欢原版。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1563 -->
# 六级单词 1563 · 声音的；直言的
中文：adj. 声音的，发声的，口头的；畅所欲言的，直言的 n. (爵士乐或流行音乐的)歌唱部分
单词：###vocal###
搭配：###vocal cords### 声带
例句：When a film is dubbed, the hero's sarcasm, ###vocal### tone and subtle humour are often lost, so many viewers prefer the original.
译文：电影一旦配音，主角的挖苦、声音语气和微妙的幽默往往就没了，所以很多观众更喜欢原版。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1442 -->
# 六级单词 1442 · 微妙的；精细的
中文：adj. 微细的，微妙的，难以察觉的；精巧的，精妙的；敏感的，敏锐的；精细的
单词：###subtle###
搭配：###a subtle difference### 细微的差别
例句：When a film is dubbed, the hero's sarcasm, vocal tone and ###subtle### humour are often lost, so many viewers prefer the original.
译文：电影一旦配音，主角的挖苦、声音语气和微妙的幽默往往就没了，所以很多观众更喜欢原版。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0568 -->
# 六级单词 0568 · 铰链，合页；取决于
中文：n. 铰链，枢纽；关键，转折点；合页 v. 装上铰链，用铰链接合；取决于
单词：###hinge###
搭配：###hinge on### 取决于
例句：The door makes a noise! — The ###hinge### needs oil. — Can you lubricate it? — Sure, and I'll tighten the lever that hangs loosely.
译文：门吱吱响！——合页需要上油了。——你能给它润滑一下吗？——行，我顺便把那根松垮垮的拉杆紧一紧。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0569 -->
# 六级单词 0569 · 润滑
中文：v. 使润滑，给…上油；起润滑作用
单词：###lubricate###
搭配：###lubricate the engine### 给发动机加润滑油
例句：The door makes a noise! — The hinge needs oil. — Can you ###lubricate### it? — Sure, and I'll tighten the lever that hangs loosely.
译文：门吱吱响！——合页需要上油了。——你能给它润滑一下吗？——行，我顺便把那根松垮垮的拉杆紧一紧。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w2266 -->
# 六级单词 2266 · 杠杆；控制杆；撬动
中文：n. 杠杆；手段；控制杆 v. 用杠杆撬动
单词：###lever###
搭配：###pull a lever### 拉动控制杆
例句：The door makes a noise! — The hinge needs oil. — Can you lubricate it? — Sure, and I'll tighten the ###lever### that hangs loosely.
译文：门吱吱响！——合页需要上油了。——你能给它润滑一下吗？——行，我顺便把那根松垮垮的拉杆紧一紧。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1294 -->
# 六级单词 1294 · 松散地；不严格地
中文：adv. 松松地，松散地；不严格地
单词：###loosely###
搭配：###loosely defined### 定义宽泛的
例句：The door makes a noise! — The hinge needs oil. — Can you lubricate it? — Sure, and I'll tighten the lever that hangs ###loosely###.
译文：门吱吱响！——合页需要上油了。——你能给它润滑一下吗？——行，我顺便把那根松垮垮的拉杆紧一紧。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0922 -->
# 六级单词 0922 · 点燃，着火
中文：v. (使)燃烧，点燃；着火
单词：###ignite###
搭配：###ignite a fire### 点火
例句：Realizing the fuel had ###ignited###, the pilot instinctively ejected and landed safely by parachute.
译文：意识到燃油起火后，飞行员本能地弹射出舱，靠降落伞安全落地。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0923 -->

搭配：###react instinctively### 本能地做出反应
例句：Realizing the fuel had ignited, the pilot ###instinctively### ejected and landed safely by parachute.
译文：意识到燃油起火后，飞行员本能地弹射出舱，靠降落伞安全落地。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

单词：###eject###
搭配：###eject from the plane### 从飞机上弹射
例句：Realizing the fuel had ignited, the pilot instinctively ###ejected### and landed safely by parachute.
译文：意识到燃油起火后，飞行员本能地弹射出舱，靠降落伞安全落地。

搭配：###land by parachute### 跳伞降落
例句：Realizing the fuel had ignited, the pilot instinctively ejected and landed safely by ###parachute###.

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0926 -->
# 六级单词 0926 · （时间）流逝
中文：n. (时间)过去，消逝 v. (时间)过去，消逝；（时间）流逝
单词：###elapse###
搭配：###ten minutes elapsed### 过了十分钟
例句：Twenty minutes have ###elapsed### and still no bus; service is intermittent on Sundays, and the bureau's inaction over such irregularity drives me crazy.
译文：已经过去二十分钟了，公交车还没来；周日的班次时有时无，交通局对这种不准点无动于衷，真让我抓狂。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0927 -->

单词：###intermittent###
搭配：###intermittent rain### 时断时续的雨
例句：Twenty minutes have elapsed and still no bus; service is ###intermittent### on Sundays, and the bureau's inaction over such irregularity drives me crazy.
译文：已经过去二十分钟了，公交车还没来；周日的班次时有时无，交通局对这种不准点无动于衷，真让我抓狂。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0928 -->

搭配：###government inaction### 政府不作为
例句：Twenty minutes have elapsed and still no bus; service is intermittent on Sundays, and the bureau's ###inaction### over such irregularity drives me crazy.
译文：已经过去二十分钟了，公交车还没来；周日的班次时有时无，交通局对这种不准点无动于衷，真让我抓狂。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0929 -->

单词：###irregularity###
搭配：###irregularity in timing### 时间不规律

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w2267 -->
# 六级单词 2267 · 特许经营权；选举权
中文：n. 特许权；选举权；经销权；特许经营权 vt. 特许
单词：###franchise###
搭配：###a franchise store### 加盟店
例句：Before buying a ###franchise###, investors should gauge local demand, a prerequisite for success, since hidden fees can jeopardize profits.
译文：买特许经营权之前，投资者应该评估当地需求，这是成功的前提，因为隐藏费用会危及利润。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w2268 -->

搭配：###gauge the mood### 判断情绪
例句：Before buying a franchise, investors should ###gauge### local demand, a prerequisite for success, since hidden fees can jeopardize profits.
译文：买特许经营权之前，投资者应该评估当地需求，这是成功的前提，因为隐藏费用会危及利润。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w2269 -->

搭配：###a prerequisite for### 的前提
例句：Before buying a franchise, investors should gauge local demand, a ###prerequisite### for success, since hidden fees can jeopardize profits.

例句：###Geographical### isolation kept the isle's culture homogeneous for centuries, but tourism now brings a vogue for foreign food.

# 六级单词 0431 · 单脚跳；跳上；跳

单词：###hop###
搭配：###hop on a bus### 跳上公交车
例句：Can you ###hop### on one leg? — Sure. — Now run in a zigzag and leap that hurdle. — No way, I'll stumble!
译文：你能单脚跳吗？——当然。——现在跑个之字形，再跨过那个栏架。——不行，我会绊倒的！

搭配：###in a zigzag### 呈之字形
例句：Can you hop on one leg? — Sure. — Now run in a ###zigzag### and leap that hurdle. — No way, I'll stumble!
译文：你能单脚跳吗？——当然。——现在跑个之字形，再跨过那个栏架。——不行，我会绊倒的！

# 六级单词 2290 · 即兴的

例句：Why so nervous? — I must give an ###impromptu### speech. — Ponder three points first. — What if I stammer? — Breathe; keeping calm is imperative.

搭配：###inaccessible areas### 交通不便的地区

译文：长期无所事事会消磨士气，所以监狱开设课程，帮助不识字的犯人学认字。

# 六级单词 0919 · 不宽容的；不耐……的

单词：###intolerant###
搭配：###lactose intolerant### 乳糖不耐受
例句：Why is he so ###intolerant###? — Any view unlike his own irritates him. — What a rigid mindset! — It intrigues me how he formed that stance.
译文：他为什么这么容不得别人？——只要观点和他不同，他就恼火。——思维真僵化！——我很好奇他是怎么形成这种立场的。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- /leiji-note -->

例句：Can you ###invert### this image and zoom in on the face? — Sure. — Make the background opaque too. — Done; it looks like an inversion of reality!

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0990 -->
# 六级单词 0990 · 包含；把……合并
