单词：###incorporate###
搭配：###incorporate A into B### 把 A 纳入 B
例句：###Incorporating### solar panels may incur higher initial costs, but the savings in kilowatt hours will outweigh them within a decade.

单词：###prosecute###
搭配：###be prosecuted for### 因……被起诉
例句：The banker was indicted for fraud, and prosecutors plan to ###prosecute### his partners too, as indignant citizens voice their indignation online.
译文：那名银行家因诈骗被起诉，检察官还打算起诉他的合伙人，愤慨的市民纷纷在网上表达不满。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w2314 -->

例句：The banker was indicted for fraud, and prosecutors plan to prosecute his partners too, as ###indignant### citizens voice their indignation online.

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1394 -->
# 六级单词 1394 · 合法的；正当的
中文：adj. 合法的，法律认可的；合乎逻辑的；正当的 v. 使合法
单词：###legitimate###
搭配：###a legitimate reason### 正当理由
例句：Is this email ###legitimate###? It asks for my bank details. — Sounds sinister! Banks never solicit details by email; send the bank a query.
译文：这封邮件是正规的吗？它要我的银行信息。——听着就不对劲！银行从不通过邮件索要信息，你向银行问一下吧。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w2315 -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1344 -->
# 六级单词 1344 · 不加区别地；任意地
中文：adv. 不加选择地，不加区分地；任意地
单词：###indiscriminately###
搭配：###fire indiscriminately### 胡乱开枪
例句：Chemicals sprayed ###indiscriminately### kill a broad spectrum of insects irrespective of whether they are pests, and the damage is far from negligible.
译文：乱喷的化学药剂不管是不是害虫，都会杀死各种各样的昆虫，造成的损害绝不能忽视。

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1091 -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0917 -->
# 六级单词 0917 · 惯性；惰性
中文：n. 无活力，迟钝，懒惰；惰性，保守，惯性
单词：###inertia###
搭配：###overcome inertia### 克服惰性
例句：Bureaucratic ###inertia### often stifles reform, yet the need to restructure the pension system is inescapable as the population ages.
译文：官僚惯性常常扼杀改革，但随着人口老龄化，改革养老金制度已是不可回避的需要。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1603 -->
# 六级单词 1603 · 相似；肖像
中文：n. 同样；类似，相似；肖像
单词：###likeness###
搭配：###a good likeness### 画得很像
例句：Is this portrait a good ###likeness###? — Very! It even captures her stern look. — Who painted it? — A noted artist; the frame is carved from marble.
译文：这幅肖像画得像吗？——很像！连她严肃的神情都画出来了。——是谁画的？——一位知名画家，画框是大理石雕的。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w2322 -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0931 -->
# 六级单词 0931 · 无穷无尽的
中文：adj. 用不完的，无穷无尽的
单词：###inexhaustible###
搭配：###an inexhaustible supply### 无穷无尽的供应
例句：Solar energy is practically ###inexhaustible###, and photoelectric panels are now cheap, but storing power remains infinitely harder, so the technology is not yet viable everywhere.
译文：太阳能几乎取之不尽，光电板现在也便宜了，但储电仍然难得多，所以这项技术还不能处处可行。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1707 -->

译文：太阳能几乎取之不尽，光电板现在也便宜了，但储电仍然难得多，所以这项技术还不能处处可行。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1353 -->

<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w2324 -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0735 -->
# 六级单词 0735 · 奉承，讨好

例句：Some commentators ###flatter### the regime, whereas others scorn its ideological rigidity and warn of its instability.

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w2326 -->
# 六级单词 2326 · 环；循环；使成环
中文：n. 圈，环，环孔；循环 v. 使成环
单词：###loop###
搭配：###on a loop### 循环播放
例句：This tune has been on a ###loop### all day! — And you keep murmuring it. — It breaks the monotony. — Not for me; it makes me yawn!
译文：这首曲子循环了一整天！——你还一直哼哼。——它能打破单调。——我可不觉得，听得我直打哈欠！
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1587 -->

单词：###murmur###
搭配：###murmur a reply### 低声回答
例句：This tune has been on a loop all day! — And you keep ###murmuring### it. — It breaks the monotony. — Not for me; it makes me yawn!
译文：这首曲子循环了一整天！——你还一直哼哼。——它能打破单调。——我可不觉得，听得我直打哈欠！
<!-- /leiji-note -->

中文：n. 单调，千篇一律
单词：###monotony###
搭配：###break the monotony### 打破单调
例句：This tune has been on a loop all day! — And you keep murmuring it. — It breaks the ###monotony###. — Not for me; it makes me yawn!
译文：这首曲子循环了一整天！——你还一直哼哼。——它能打破单调。——我可不觉得，听得我直打哈欠！
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w2328 -->

单词：###infringe###
搭配：###infringe copyright### 侵犯版权
例句：When a website copied her manuscript and ###infringed### her copyright, the author decided to sue, although the site would plead ignorance.
译文：一家网站抄袭了她的书稿、侵犯了她的版权，作者决定起诉，尽管网站会推说不知情。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

单词：###restless###
搭配：###a restless night### 不眠之夜
例句：Why is the TV on mute? — I've just soothed the baby; she was ###restless### all night. — Then I'll read quietly in the suite upstairs.
译文：电视怎么静音了？——我刚把宝宝哄好，她一晚上都不安稳。——那我去楼上的套房里安静地看书。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0121 -->
# 六级单词 0121 · 套房；一套家具

单词：###suite###
搭配：###a hotel suite### 酒店套房
例句：Why is the TV on mute? — I've just soothed the baby; she was restless all night. — Then I'll read quietly in the ###suite### upstairs.
译文：电视怎么静音了？——我刚把宝宝哄好，她一晚上都不安稳。——那我去楼上的套房里安静地看书。

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w2362 -->
# 六级单词 2362 · 纵向的；经度的
中文：adj. 纵向的；经度的
单词：###longitudinal###
搭配：###a longitudinal study### 纵向研究
例句：A ###longitudinal### study tracked children for decades and found that early poverty may underlie later physiological and psychiatric problems.
译文：一项纵向研究跟踪儿童几十年后发现，早年贫困可能是日后生理和精神问题的根源。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1231 -->
# 六级单词 1231 · 鲁莽的，不顾后果的

搭配：###reckless driving### 鲁莽驾驶
例句：My ###reckless### brother dropped this record, so now there's a scratch in the groove: it hisses, skips, and the speaker rattles.
译文：我那个冒失的弟弟把这张唱片摔了，现在纹道上有道划痕：放起来嘶嘶作响、跳针，连音箱都在嗡嗡响。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1232 -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w2365 -->

搭配：###obscene profits### 暴利
例句：Seen that film? — Some scenes were almost ###obscene###. — It caused a sensation at the festival. — I only skimmed the reviews; I hate sensational plots.
译文：看过那部电影吗？——有些镜头近乎下流。——它在电影节上引起了轰动。——我只粗略看了看影评，我讨厌哗众取宠的情节。

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1070 -->

单词：###mismanagement###
搭配：###financial mismanagement### 财务管理不善
例句：Years of ###mismanagement### left the agency in chaos, and auditors found nominal accounts hiding unethical deals with a syndicate.
译文：多年管理不善让这家机构一片混乱，审计人员发现了一些挂名账户，背后隐藏着与某个犯罪集团的不道德交易。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w2371 -->

单词：###obsess###
搭配：###be obsessed with### 痴迷于

译文：你怎么这么迷那个游戏？——它让我特别兴奋。——你一整天都在想着它！——就再玩一个单人任务。

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0674 -->

搭配：###personal magnetism### 个人魅力
例句：Long before satellites, sailors relied on ###magnetism### to navigate, maneuvering ships with sails and oars through turbulent seas.

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1493 -->

例句：Long before satellites, sailors relied on magnetism to navigate, ###maneuvering### ships with sails and oars through turbulent seas.

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w2375 -->

搭配：###turbulent times### 动荡的年代
例句：Long before satellites, sailors relied on magnetism to navigate, maneuvering ships with sails and oars through ###turbulent### seas.

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1259 -->
# 六级单词 1259 · 纬度
中文：n. 纬度，纬线
单词：###latitude###
搭配：###high latitudes### 高纬度地区
例句：Places at the same ###latitude### but in different hemispheres, even near the tropics, have opposite seasons whatever their longitude.
译文：纬度相同但分处南北半球的地方，即使靠近热带，无论经度如何，季节都正好相反。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1260 -->

搭配：###the northern hemisphere### 北半球
例句：Places at the same latitude but in different ###hemispheres###, even near the tropics, have opposite seasons whatever their longitude.
译文：纬度相同但分处南北半球的地方，即使靠近热带，无论经度如何，季节都正好相反。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1261 -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1262 -->
# 六级单词 1262 · 经度
中文：n. 经度
单词：###longitude###
搭配：###lines of longitude### 经线
例句：Places at the same latitude but in different hemispheres, even near the tropics, have opposite seasons whatever their ###longitude###.
译文：纬度相同但分处南北半球的地方，即使靠近热带，无论经度如何，季节都正好相反。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w2376 -->
# 六级单词 2376 · 开始；发作

单词：###onset###
搭配：###the onset of winter### 冬天的来临
例句：When was the ###onset### of your fever? — Last night, and now there's a rash on my neck. — You're quivering! — I feel physically exhausted.
译文：你什么时候开始发烧的？——昨晚，现在脖子上还起了疹子。——你在发抖！——我觉得浑身没力气。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

# 六级单词 2377 · 损坏；破坏

单词：###mar###
搭配：###mar the beauty of### 破坏……的美
例句：Rumours could not ###mar### the name of the renowned surgeon, whose skill was manifest in the lives she saved at a reputable hospital.
译文：谣言无损这位著名外科医生的名声，她在一家声誉卓著的医院救过的众多生命就是她医术的明证。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

# 六级单词 2384 · 疏忽的
中文：adj. 忽略的；疏忽的；粗心大意的
单词：###negligent###
搭配：###be negligent in### 在……方面疏忽
例句：A ###negligent### driver may face prosecution notwithstanding his excuses, especially if witnesses testify that his obstruction of traffic endangered lives.
译文：一个疏忽大意的司机不管有什么借口都可能被起诉，尤其是在证人证明他阻碍交通危及他人生命的情况下。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- /leiji-note -->

译文：狗为什么一直嗅来嗅去、挠个不停？——也许身上有寄生虫。——可怜的小家伙，看着真可怜。——我们把它放进浴缸好好刷一刷吧。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1471 -->
# 六级单词 1471 · 接近，邻近
中文：n. 接近，邻近
单词：###proximity###
搭配：###in close proximity### 非常接近
例句：Staring at screens in close ###proximity### for hours may strain the optic nerve and potentially impair vision, so some employers stipulate hourly breaks.
译文：近距离盯着屏幕几个小时可能使视神经疲劳，还可能损害视力，所以有些雇主规定每小时休息一次。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

单词：###masculine###
搭配：###a masculine voice### 男性化的嗓音
例句：The belief that pink is less ###masculine### than blue is a senseless stereotype, since a colour preference is not a fixed trait.
译文：认为粉色不如蓝色有男子气，是一种毫无道理的刻板印象，因为喜欢什么颜色并不是固定的性格特征。

例句：Can you ###poke### the fire? — Sure, and I'll shove another log in. — Snap some small sticks first. — Good idea; what a tranquil evening!
译文：你能拨一拨火吗？——行，我再塞一根木头进去。——先折几根小树枝。——好主意，多宁静的夜晚啊！

单词：###testimony###
搭配：###give testimony### 作证
例句：Inasmuch as the key ###testimony### was flawed, the verdict, though unanimous, may be overturned on appeal.
译文：由于关键证词有缺陷，这一裁决虽然是全票通过的，在上诉时仍可能被推翻。

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1605 -->
# 六级单词 1605 · 连续的；连载的；连续剧

单词：###serial###
搭配：###a serial killer### 连环杀手
例句：What's the film's premise? — A robot falls in love, based on an online ###serial###. — Sounds questionable. — Trust me, the plot unfolds beautifully.
译文：这部电影的设定是什么？——一个机器人坠入爱河，改编自网上的连载。——听着有点悬。——相信我，剧情展开得很精彩。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1230 -->
# 六级单词 1230 · 分子的
中文：adj. 分子的；克分子的
单词：###molecular###
搭配：###molecular biology### 分子生物学
例句：###Molecular### biologists study how each molecule interacts within a cell, and their simulations may revolutionize drug design.
译文：分子生物学家研究细胞中每个分子如何相互作用，他们的模拟可能会彻底改变药物设计。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0371 -->
# 六级单词 0371 · 君主

搭配：###an absolute monarch### 专制君主
例句：In Britain, a constitutional monarchy, the ###monarch### has little real power; the current king's reign began when he took the throne in 2022.
译文：英国是君主立宪制国家，君主几乎没有实权；现任国王于 2022 年登基，开始了他的统治。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

例句：Is your brother a ###psychiatrist###? — Yes; he studied psychiatry and just won tenure at the university. — Impressive! — He says patients' subconscious fears fascinate him.
译文：你哥哥是精神科医生吗？——是的，他学的是精神病学，刚在大学拿到终身教职。——真厉害！——他说病人潜意识里的恐惧让他着迷。

# 六级单词 2420 · 推定；假定
中文：n. 推定；假定
单词：###presumption###
搭配：###the presumption of innocence### 无罪推定
例句：Under the ###presumption### of innocence, police need a warrant to search a home, and prosecutors must establish guilt with rigor, whereby justice is safeguarded.
译文：在无罪推定原则下，警方搜查住宅需要搜查令，检方必须严格证明有罪，正义由此得到保障。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w2425 -->
# 六级单词 2425 · 光学的；视觉的
中文：adj. 视力的，视觉的；光学的
单词：###optical###
搭配：###an optical illusion### 视错觉
例句：Astronomers at the observatory use ###optical### telescopes to observe the outermost planets, whose orbits are perpetual and predictable.
译文：天文台的天文学家用光学望远镜观测最外层的行星，它们的轨道永恒不变，可以预测。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1538 -->
# 六级单词 1538 · 最外层的
中文：adj. 最外面的，最远的；最外层的
单词：###outermost###
搭配：###the outermost layer### 最外层
例句：Astronomers at the observatory use optical telescopes to observe the ###outermost### planets, whose orbits are perpetual and predictable.
译文：天文台的天文学家用光学望远镜观测最外层的行星，它们的轨道永恒不变，可以预测。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0306 -->
# 六级单词 0306 · 永久的；不断的
中文：adj. 永远的，永恒的，不断的；四季开花的；永久的
单词：###perpetual###
搭配：###perpetual motion### 永动
例句：Astronomers at the observatory use optical telescopes to observe the outermost planets, whose orbits are ###perpetual### and predictable.
译文：天文台的天文学家用光学望远镜观测最外层的行星，它们的轨道永恒不变，可以预测。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1762 -->
# 六级单词 1762 · 说唱乐；敲击；说唱；敲
中文：n. (轻快的)敲击声；唠叨；说唱乐 v. (轻而快地)敲击(某物)；责备或训斥(某人)；唠叨
单词：###rap###
搭配：###rap music### 说唱音乐
例句：Do you like ###rap###? — Love it! My son raps in a hip-hop subculture. — He used to be so timid! — Now he loves the spotlight.
译文：你喜欢说唱吗？——超爱！我儿子在玩嘻哈亚文化，唱说唱。——他以前那么胆小！——现在可喜欢出风头了。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1630 -->

<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1380 -->
# 六级单词 1380 · 交往，往来
中文：n. 交往，交际，交流；往来
单词：###intercourse###
搭配：###commercial intercourse### 商业往来
例句：In ancient ports, commercial ###intercourse### among merchants, pilgrims and missionaries spread merchandise and ideas across continents.
译文：在古代港口，商人、朝圣者和传教士之间的商业往来，把商品和思想传播到各个大陆。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

例句：In ancient ports, commercial intercourse among merchants, ###pilgrims### and missionaries spread merchandise and ideas across continents.

例句：In ancient ports, commercial intercourse among merchants, pilgrims and ###missionaries### spread merchandise and ideas across continents.

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w2428 -->
# 六级单词 2428 · 最接近的；直接的
中文：adj. 最接近的；直接的
单词：###proximate###
搭配：###the proximate cause### 直接原因
例句：The ###proximate### cause of the power cut was a failed transistor, though the underlying problem, which engineers must now rectify, was years of neglect.
译文：停电的直接原因是一个晶体管坏了，不过深层问题是多年来疏于维护，工程师现在必须加以纠正。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w2429 -->

搭配：###a transistor radio### 晶体管收音机

搭配：###the underlying cause### 根本原因
例句：The proximate cause of the power cut was a failed transistor, though the ###underlying### problem, which engineers must now rectify, was years of neglect.

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w2430 -->
# 六级单词 2430 · 震惊；蹒跚；卷绕；卷轴
中文：n. 线轴，卷盘；卷轴 v. 卷，绕；震惊；蹒跚；卷绕
单词：###reel###
搭配：###reel from the shock### 被震惊得晕头转向
例句：I'm still ###reeling### from the news. — What news? — The boss terminated our project. — That's so unreasonable; I yearned to see it finished!
译文：我还没从这个消息里回过神来。——什么消息？——老板把我们的项目叫停了。——太不讲道理了，我多想看到它完成啊！
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1783 -->

搭配：###terminate a contract### 终止合同
例句：I'm still reeling from the news. — What news? — The boss ###terminated### our project. — That's so unreasonable; I yearned to see it finished!
译文：我还没从这个消息里回过神来。——什么消息？——老板把我们的项目叫停了。——太不讲道理了，我多想看到它完成啊！
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1739 -->

单词：###unreasonable###
搭配：###an unreasonable demand### 无理要求
例句：I'm still reeling from the news. — What news? — The boss terminated our project. — That's so ###unreasonable###; I yearned to see it finished!
译文：我还没从这个消息里回过神来。——什么消息？——老板把我们的项目叫停了。——太不讲道理了，我多想看到它完成啊！
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1481 -->

搭配：###yearn for### 渴望
例句：I'm still reeling from the news. — What news? — The boss terminated our project. — That's so unreasonable; I ###yearned### to see it finished!
译文：我还没从这个消息里回过神来。——什么消息？——老板把我们的项目叫停了。——太不讲道理了，我多想看到它完成啊！
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w2431 -->
# 六级单词 2431 · 起作用的；生效的；工人；特工
中文：adj. 起作用的；生效的 n. 工人；特工
单词：###operative###
搭配：###become operative### 开始生效
例句：Although the new law became ###operative### last year, old customs still predominate in villages, and its impact is not easily quantifiable, only proximately estimated.
译文：新法虽然去年就已生效，但在乡村仍是旧习俗占上风，而且它的影响很难量化，只能大致估计。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w2432 -->

单词：###predominate###
搭配：###predominate over### 胜过
例句：Although the new law became operative last year, old customs still ###predominate### in villages, and its impact is not easily quantifiable, only proximately estimated.
译文：新法虽然去年就已生效，但在乡村仍是旧习俗占上风，而且它的影响很难量化，只能大致估计。

搭配：###quantifiable results### 可量化的结果
例句：Although the new law became operative last year, old customs still predominate in villages, and its impact is not easily ###quantifiable###, only proximately estimated.
译文：新法虽然去年就已生效，但在乡村仍是旧习俗占上风，而且它的影响很难量化，只能大致估计。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1771 -->

中文：adv. 近似，接近；直接地
单词：###proximately###
搭配：###proximately calculated### 大致算出的
例句：Although the new law became operative last year, old customs still predominate in villages, and its impact is not easily quantifiable, only ###proximately### estimated.
译文：新法虽然去年就已生效，但在乡村仍是旧习俗占上风，而且它的影响很难量化，只能大致估计。

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1396 -->

搭配：###an intuitive interface### 直观的界面
例句：This app is so ###intuitive### that even Grandma could navigate it using the preset modes, without having to readjust anything.

搭配：###navigate a website### 浏览网站
例句：This app is so intuitive that even Grandma could ###navigate### it using the preset modes, without having to readjust anything.

搭配：###oppress the poor### 压迫穷人

搭配：###an infantry regiment### 步兵团
例句：Did your grandpa serve in a horse ###regiment###? — Yes; he could rein in a horse without a saddle. — Amazing! — He still salutes the flag daily.
译文：你爷爷在骑兵团服过役吗？——是的，他不用马鞍就能勒住马。——太厉害了！——他现在每天还向国旗敬礼。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

搭配：###rein in### 控制

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w2438 -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1593 -->
# 六级单词 1593 · 正统的；东正教的
中文：adj. 正统的，传统的；东正教的
单词：###orthodox###
搭配：###orthodox medicine### 正统医学
例句：Her parents are ###orthodox###, but she is secular and not superstitious, though she still attends every family ritual.
译文：她父母信教很严格，她自己却不信教，也不迷信，不过每次家族仪式她都参加。

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w2439 -->

搭配：###a secular state### 世俗国家
例句：Her parents are orthodox, but she is ###secular### and not superstitious, though she still attends every family ritual.

<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w2440 -->
# 六级单词 2440 · 迷信的
中文：adj. 迷信的，受迷信思想支配的
单词：###superstitious###
搭配：###superstitious beliefs### 迷信观念
例句：Her parents are orthodox, but she is secular and not ###superstitious###, though she still attends every family ritual.
译文：她父母信教很严格，她自己却不信教，也不迷信，不过每次家族仪式她都参加。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w2441 -->
# 六级单词 2441 · 责备；责备
中文：n. 谴责，责备 v. 责备
单词：###reproach###
搭配：###beyond reproach### 无可指责
例句：Why did your wife ###reproach### you? — I forgot her birthday. — How thoughtless! Did you retort? — No; arguing would only make her retaliate.
译文：你妻子为什么责备你？——我忘了她的生日。——太粗心了！你反驳了吗？——没有，争辩只会让她反击。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1377 -->

<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1302 -->
# 六级单词 1302 · 比……活得长
中文：v. 比……活的时间长
单词：###outlive###
搭配：###outlive one's usefulness### 失去作用
例句：Women generally ###outlive### men, so residents of care homes are predominantly female, a gap researchers attribute principally to lifestyle, though regional variance is large.
译文：女性通常比男性长寿，所以养老院里的住户以女性为主，研究人员认为这一差距主要源于生活方式，不过地区之间差别很大。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- /leiji-note -->

搭配：###rig an election### 操纵选举

单词：###rotation###
搭配：###crop rotation### 轮作
例句：Why do farmers practise crop ###rotation###? — It improves the soil's texture, so crops don't wither. — Grandpa was thrifty too; he saved every seed.
译文：农民为什么要轮作？——能改善土壤质地，庄稼就不会枯萎。——爷爷也很节俭，每颗种子都留着。

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1669 -->

搭配：###overlap with### 与……重叠
例句：When departments' duties ###overlap###, managers overstate their roles and subordinates get confused, so a clear partition of tasks is essential.
译文：部门职责重叠时，经理们会夸大自己的作用，下属则无所适从，所以明确划分任务至关重要。

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1705 -->
# 六级单词 1705 · 模拟；假装
中文：v. 假装，冒充；模拟，模仿
单词：###simulate###
搭配：###simulate conditions### 模拟条件
例句：Fear can paralyze people in a crisis, so drills that ###simulate### fires help them stay sober and evacuate via the stairway.
译文：危急时刻恐惧会让人动弹不得，所以模拟火灾的演习能帮助人们保持清醒，从楼梯撤离。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w2454 -->
# 六级单词 2454 · 清醒的；未醉的
中文：adj. 未醉的，清醒的；审慎的，镇定的，严肃的
单词：###sober###
搭配：###stay sober### 保持清醒
例句：Fear can paralyze people in a crisis, so drills that simulate fires help them stay ###sober### and evacuate via the stairway.
译文：危急时刻恐惧会让人动弹不得，所以模拟火灾的演习能帮助人们保持清醒，从楼梯撤离。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0019 -->
# 六级单词 0019 · 楼梯
中文：n. 楼梯
单词：###stairway###
搭配：###a narrow stairway### 狭窄的楼梯
例句：Fear can paralyze people in a crisis, so drills that simulate fires help them stay sober and evacuate via the ###stairway###.
译文：危急时刻恐惧会让人动弹不得，所以模拟火灾的演习能帮助人们保持清醒，从楼梯撤离。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w2455 -->
# 六级单词 2455 · 刮；擦伤；刮擦
中文：v. 刮，削，擦，刮落，擦去；刮坏，擦伤；擦过，勉强通过 n. 擦伤，擦痕；刮擦
单词：###scrape###
搭配：###scrape through### 勉强通过
例句：Did you ###scrape### the car? — Unintentionally; the engine stalled by the gate. — OK, but don't slam the door like that!
译文：你把车刮了吗？——不是故意的，发动机在大门口熄火了。——好吧，但别那样摔车门！
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1621 -->
# 六级单词 1621 · 无意地
中文：adv. 无意地
单词：###unintentionally###
搭配：###hurt sb. unintentionally### 无意中伤害某人
例句：Did you scrape the car? — ###Unintentionally###; the engine stalled by the gate. — OK, but don't slam the door like that!
译文：你把车刮了吗？——不是故意的，发动机在大门口熄火了。——好吧，但别那样摔车门！
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0440 -->
# 六级单词 0440 · 熄火；拖延；货摊
中文：n. 货摊；畜栏，厩 v. (使)停转，(使)停止；熄火；拖延
单词：###stall###
搭配：###stall for time### 拖延时间
例句：Did you scrape the car? — Unintentionally; the engine ###stalled### by the gate. — OK, but don't slam the door like that!
译文：你把车刮了吗？——不是故意的，发动机在大门口熄火了。——好吧，但别那样摔车门！
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w2456 -->
# 六级单词 2456 · 砰地关上；猛烈抨击
中文：vt. 使劲关，砰地放下；砰地关上；猛烈抨击
单词：###slam###
搭配：###slam the door### 砰地关门
例句：Did you scrape the car? — Unintentionally; the engine stalled by the gate. — OK, but don't ###slam### the door like that!
译文：你把车刮了吗？——不是故意的，发动机在大门口熄火了。——好吧，但别那样摔车门！
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0305 -->
# 六级单词 0305 · 钟摆
中文：n. 摆，钟摆；摇摆不定的事态
单词：###pendulum###
搭配：###the swing of the pendulum### 钟摆的摆动
例句：Like a ###pendulum###, public opinion swings between extremes, and politicians use rhetorical tricks to exploit the polarity and perplexity of voters.
译文：舆论像钟摆一样在两个极端之间摆动，政客们用修辞手段利用选民的两极分化和困惑。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1463 -->

中文：adj. 修辞的；反问的
单词：###rhetorical###
搭配：###a rhetorical question### 反问句
例句：Like a pendulum, public opinion swings between extremes, and politicians use ###rhetorical### tricks to exploit the polarity and perplexity of voters.
译文：舆论像钟摆一样在两个极端之间摆动，政客们用修辞手段利用选民的两极分化和困惑。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1410 -->
# 六级单词 1410 · 两极；对立
中文：n. （人、意见或观念的）截然相反，截然对立；极性；两极
单词：###polarity###
搭配：###political polarity### 政治两极分化
例句：Like a pendulum, public opinion swings between extremes, and politicians use rhetorical tricks to exploit the ###polarity### and perplexity of voters.
译文：舆论像钟摆一样在两个极端之间摆动，政客们用修辞手段利用选民的两极分化和困惑。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w2457 -->
# 六级单词 2457 · 困惑
中文：n. 困惑
单词：###perplexity###
搭配：###in perplexity### 困惑地
例句：Like a pendulum, public opinion swings between extremes, and politicians use rhetorical tricks to exploit the polarity and ###perplexity### of voters.
译文：舆论像钟摆一样在两个极端之间摆动，政客们用修辞手段利用选民的两极分化和困惑。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w2458 -->
# 六级单词 2458 · 抒情的；歌词；抒情诗
中文：adj. 抒情的 n. 抒情诗；歌词
单词：###lyric###
搭配：###lyric poetry### 抒情诗
例句：When translating ###lyric### poetry, a translator who ignores intonation and rhetoric will produce lines that are intelligible but lifeless.
译文：翻译抒情诗时，忽视语调和修辞的译者译出的诗句虽然看得懂，却毫无生气。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0061 -->

中文：n. 语调，声调；发声
单词：###intonation###
搭配：###rising intonation### 升调
例句：When translating lyric poetry, a translator who ignores ###intonation### and rhetoric will produce lines that are intelligible but lifeless.
译文：翻译抒情诗时，忽视语调和修辞的译者译出的诗句虽然看得懂，却毫无生气。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w2459 -->
# 六级单词 2459 · 修辞；花言巧语
中文：n. 雄辩言辞，修辞学；花言巧语
单词：###rhetoric###
搭配：###empty rhetoric### 空话
例句：When translating lyric poetry, a translator who ignores intonation and ###rhetoric### will produce lines that are intelligible but lifeless.
译文：翻译抒情诗时，忽视语调和修辞的译者译出的诗句虽然看得懂，却毫无生气。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0901 -->
# 六级单词 0901 · 可理解的
中文：adj. 可理解的，易于明白的
单词：###intelligible###
搭配：###barely intelligible### 几乎听不懂的
例句：When translating lyric poetry, a translator who ignores intonation and rhetoric will produce lines that are ###intelligible### but lifeless.
译文：翻译抒情诗时，忽视语调和修辞的译者译出的诗句虽然看得懂，却毫无生气。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1464 -->

中文：n. 猩红色 adj. 猩红的
单词：###scarlet###
搭配：###a scarlet dress### 猩红色的连衣裙
例句：That ###scarlet### lipstick goes perfectly with your outfit, and it looks elegant rather than vulgar.
译文：那支猩红色的口红跟你这身衣服很配，看起来很优雅，一点也不俗气。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1465 -->

中文：n. 唇膏，口红

搭配：###wear lipstick### 涂口红
例句：That scarlet ###lipstick### goes perfectly with your outfit, and it looks elegant rather than vulgar.
译文：那支猩红色的口红跟你这身衣服很配，看起来很优雅，一点也不俗气。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1466 -->

中文：n. 全套设备或用具；全套衣装 v. 为…提供设备
单词：###outfit###
搭配：###a new outfit### 一套新衣服
例句：That scarlet lipstick goes perfectly with your ###outfit###, and it looks elegant rather than vulgar.
译文：那支猩红色的口红跟你这身衣服很配，看起来很优雅，一点也不俗气。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1467 -->
# 六级单词 1467 · 庸俗的，粗俗的
中文：adj. 粗鲁的，卑下的；庸俗的，低级的；粗俗的
单词：###vulgar###
搭配：###vulgar taste### 低俗的品味
例句：That scarlet lipstick goes perfectly with your outfit, and it looks elegant rather than ###vulgar###.

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w2460 -->
# 六级单词 2460 · 石油化工的；石化产品
中文：adj. 石化的；石油化工的 n. 石化产品
单词：###petrochemical###
搭配：###the petrochemical industry### 石化工业
例句：The ###petrochemical### plant is the predominant employer, so closing it would leave the town stagnant, a painful tradeoff between jobs and health.
译文：那家石化厂是当地主要雇主，关掉它会让小镇停滞不前，这是就业与健康之间痛苦的取舍。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1280 -->

中文：adj. 占优势的，占主导地位的，最显著的；主要的
单词：###predominant###
搭配：###the predominant colour### 主色调
例句：The petrochemical plant is the ###predominant### employer, so closing it would leave the town stagnant, a painful tradeoff between jobs and health.
译文：那家石化厂是当地主要雇主，关掉它会让小镇停滞不前，这是就业与健康之间痛苦的取舍。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

单词：###stagnant###
搭配：###stagnant water### 死水
例句：The petrochemical plant is the predominant employer, so closing it would leave the town ###stagnant###, a painful tradeoff between jobs and health.
译文：那家石化厂是当地主要雇主，关掉它会让小镇停滞不前，这是就业与健康之间痛苦的取舍。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1753 -->

中文：n. 权衡；取舍
单词：###tradeoff###
搭配：###a tradeoff between### 在……之间的取舍
例句：The petrochemical plant is the predominant employer, so closing it would leave the town stagnant, a painful ###tradeoff### between jobs and health.
译文：那家石化厂是当地主要雇主，关掉它会让小镇停滞不前，这是就业与健康之间痛苦的取舍。

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1181 -->
# 六级单词 1181 · 粉碎；破坏
中文：v. 粉碎，损毁；破坏
单词：###shatter###
搭配：###shatter one's dreams### 使梦想破灭
例句：Did a ball ###shatter### the window? — Yes; the kids snatched it and ran. — Don't tread on the glass! — I'm too weary to scold them.
译文：窗户是被球砸碎的吗？——是的，孩子们抢回球就跑了。——别踩到玻璃！——我累得都懒得说他们了。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1802 -->

单词：###tread###
搭配：###tread on### 踩到
例句：Did a ball shatter the window? — Yes; the kids snatched it and ran. — Don't ###tread### on the glass! — I'm too weary to scold them.
译文：窗户是被球砸碎的吗？——是的，孩子们抢回球就跑了。——别踩到玻璃！——我累得都懒得说他们了。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1447 -->
# 六级单词 1447 · 抢夺；抓住；片段

单词：###snatch###
搭配：###snatch a chance### 抓住机会
例句：Did a ball shatter the window? — Yes; the kids ###snatched### it and ran. — Don't tread on the glass! — I'm too weary to scold them.
译文：窗户是被球砸碎的吗？——是的，孩子们抢回球就跑了。——别踩到玻璃！——我累得都懒得说他们了。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w2462 -->

中文：adj. 非常疲倦的；厌烦的
单词：###weary###
搭配：###weary of### 厌倦
例句：Did a ball shatter the window? — Yes; the kids snatched it and ran. — Don't tread on the glass! — I'm too ###weary### to scold them.
译文：窗户是被球砸碎的吗？——是的，孩子们抢回球就跑了。——别踩到玻璃！——我累得都懒得说他们了。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1619 -->
# 六级单词 1619 · 在……之前
中文：v. 先于…，位于…之前；在……之前
单词：###precede###
搭配：###precede sth. by### 比……早
例句：Warning signs ###preceded### the crash: in the preceding years, reckless lending soared, and economists who refuted unrealistic forecasts were ridiculed.
译文：崩盘之前就有预警信号：在此前几年，鲁莽的放贷激增，驳斥不切实际预测的经济学家却遭到嘲笑。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w2463 -->
# 六级单词 2463 · 在前的
中文：adj. 在前的；在先的
单词：###preceding###
搭配：###the preceding chapter### 前一章
例句：Warning signs preceded the crash: in the ###preceding### years, reckless lending soared, and economists who refuted unrealistic forecasts were ridiculed.
译文：崩盘之前就有预警信号：在此前几年，鲁莽的放贷激增，驳斥不切实际预测的经济学家却遭到嘲笑。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w2464 -->

单词：###refute###
搭配：###refute a claim### 驳斥某种说法
例句：Warning signs preceded the crash: in the preceding years, reckless lending soared, and economists who ###refuted### unrealistic forecasts were ridiculed.
译文：崩盘之前就有预警信号：在此前几年，鲁莽的放贷激增，驳斥不切实际预测的经济学家却遭到嘲笑。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1741 -->

中文：adj. 不现实的；不切实际的
单词：###unrealistic###
搭配：###unrealistic expectations### 不切实际的期望
例句：Warning signs preceded the crash: in the preceding years, reckless lending soared, and economists who refuted ###unrealistic### forecasts were ridiculed.
译文：崩盘之前就有预警信号：在此前几年，鲁莽的放贷激增，驳斥不切实际预测的经济学家却遭到嘲笑。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1489 -->
# 六级单词 1489 · 造船业
中文：n. 造船（业），造船学
单词：###shipbuilding###
搭配：###the shipbuilding industry### 造船业
例句：Is your dad in ###shipbuilding###? — Yes, he builds tankers at the port. — Does he design submarines too? — No, submarines are another specialty.
译文：你爸爸是造船的吗？——是的，他在港口造油轮。——他也设计潜艇吗？——不，潜艇是另一门专业。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1275 -->

搭配：###an oil tanker### 油轮
例句：Is your dad in shipbuilding? — Yes, he builds ###tankers### at the port. — Does he design submarines too? — No, submarines are another specialty.
译文：你爸爸是造船的吗？——是的，他在港口造油轮。——他也设计潜艇吗？——不，潜艇是另一门专业。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w2465 -->

搭配：###a nuclear submarine### 核潜艇
例句：Is your dad in shipbuilding? — Yes, he builds tankers at the port. — Does he design ###submarines### too? — No, submarines are another specialty.
译文：你爸爸是造船的吗？——是的，他在港口造油轮。——他也设计潜艇吗？——不，潜艇是另一门专业。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1818 -->

搭配：###a local specialty### 地方特产
例句：Is your dad in shipbuilding? — Yes, he builds tankers at the port. — Does he design submarines too? — No, submarines are another ###specialty###.
译文：你爸爸是造船的吗？——是的，他在港口造油轮。——他也设计潜艇吗？——不，潜艇是另一门专业。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w2466 -->
# 六级单词 2466 · 掠夺性的；食肉的
中文：adj. 掠夺的，捕食生物的；掠夺性的；食肉的
单词：###predatory###
搭配：###predatory pricing### 掠夺性定价
例句：The ###predatory### bird spiralled above the field before diving like a streak at astonishing velocity.
译文：这只猛禽在田野上空盘旋，然后像一道闪电一样以惊人的速度俯冲下来。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w0531 -->
# 六级单词 0531 · 螺旋上升；螺旋
中文：n. 螺线，螺旋状的物体；不断加强的上升或下降 adj. 螺旋形的，盘旋的 v. 螺旋形移动，盘旋移动；连续增长或减少；螺旋上升
单词：###spiral###
搭配：###a downward spiral### 恶性循环
例句：The predatory bird ###spiralled### above the field before diving like a streak at astonishing velocity.
译文：这只猛禽在田野上空盘旋，然后像一道闪电一样以惊人的速度俯冲下来。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1602 -->
# 六级单词 1602 · 条纹；一连串；飞跑
中文：n. 条纹，纹理；(性格上不太好的)特质，性情，癖性；一连串 v. 在…上加条纹；飞跑
单词：###streak###
搭配：###a winning streak### 连胜
例句：The predatory bird spiralled above the field before diving like a ###streak### at astonishing velocity.
译文：这只猛禽在田野上空盘旋，然后像一道闪电一样以惊人的速度俯冲下来。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w2467 -->
# 六级单词 2467 · 速度
中文：n. (物理学中的)速度；迅速，快速
单词：###velocity###
搭配：###at high velocity### 高速
例句：The predatory bird spiralled above the field before diving like a streak at astonishing ###velocity###.
译文：这只猛禽在田野上空盘旋，然后像一道闪电一样以惊人的速度俯冲下来。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1688 -->
# 六级单词 1688 · 适于销售的
中文：adj. 适合销售的
单词：###saleable###
搭配：###a saleable product### 好卖的产品
例句：To make the product ###saleable###, the firm cut wholesale prices, and turnover subsequently doubled.
译文：为了让产品好卖，公司降低了批发价，营业额随后翻了一番。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w2468 -->
# 六级单词 2468 · 批发的；批发
中文：n. 批发 adj. 大规模的
单词：###wholesale###
搭配：###wholesale prices### 批发价
例句：To make the product saleable, the firm cut ###wholesale### prices, and turnover subsequently doubled.
译文：为了让产品好卖，公司降低了批发价，营业额随后翻了一番。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1849 -->
# 六级单词 1849 · 营业额；人员流动率
中文：n. (一定时期的)营业额；(商店的)货物周转率；人事变动率；人员流动率
单词：###turnover###
搭配：###staff turnover### 员工流动率
例句：To make the product saleable, the firm cut wholesale prices, and ###turnover### subsequently doubled.
译文：为了让产品好卖，公司降低了批发价，营业额随后翻了一番。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1607 -->
# 六级单词 1607 · 随后
中文：adv. 随后
单词：###subsequently###
搭配：###subsequently released### 随后被释放
例句：To make the product saleable, the firm cut wholesale prices, and turnover ###subsequently### doubled.
译文：为了让产品好卖，公司降低了批发价，营业额随后翻了一番。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w2469 -->
# 六级单词 2469 · 切碎；撕碎；碎片
中文：n. 碎片，细条；些许，少量 vt. 切碎，撕碎
单词：###shred###
搭配：###not a shred of### 一点也没有
例句：Should I ###shred### these old bills? — Yes; identity thieves are vicious. — And this broken wardrobe? — It's unfit for use; donate the wood.
译文：这些旧账单要用碎纸机粉碎吗？——要，盗用身份的人很凶残。——那这个坏衣柜呢？——没法用了，木头捐了吧。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1816 -->

单词：###vicious###
搭配：###a vicious circle### 恶性循环
例句：Should I shred these old bills? — Yes; identity thieves are ###vicious###. — And this broken wardrobe? — It's unfit for use; donate the wood.
译文：这些旧账单要用碎纸机粉碎吗？——要，盗用身份的人很凶残。——那这个坏衣柜呢？——没法用了，木头捐了吧。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1174 -->

单词：###wardrobe###
搭配：###a built-in wardrobe### 嵌入式衣柜
例句：Should I shred these old bills? — Yes; identity thieves are vicious. — And this broken ###wardrobe###? — It's unfit for use; donate the wood.
译文：这些旧账单要用碎纸机粉碎吗？——要，盗用身份的人很凶残。——那这个坏衣柜呢？——没法用了，木头捐了吧。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1852 -->

单词：###unfit###
搭配：###unfit for### 不适合
例句：Should I shred these old bills? — Yes; identity thieves are vicious. — And this broken wardrobe? — It's ###unfit### for use; donate the wood.

<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1744 -->
# 六级单词 1744 · 工业化以前的
中文：adj. 工业化以前的
单词：###preindustrial###
搭配：###preindustrial societies### 前工业社会
例句：In ###preindustrial### times, tribal elders settled disputes over tracts of land, and villagers were bound by reciprocal duties.
译文：在前工业时代，部落长老裁决土地纠纷，村民们之间有相互承担的义务。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w2470 -->

中文：adj. 部落的，种族的
单词：###tribal###
搭配：###tribal culture### 部落文化
例句：In preindustrial times, ###tribal### elders settled disputes over tracts of land, and villagers were bound by reciprocal duties.
译文：在前工业时代，部落长老裁决土地纠纷，村民们之间有相互承担的义务。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1121 -->
# 六级单词 1121 · 一片土地；（器官）道
中文：n. 一大片土地，地带；(人体、动物的)系统，道，束
单词：###tract###
搭配：###a tract of land### 一片土地
例句：In preindustrial times, tribal elders settled disputes over ###tracts### of land, and villagers were bound by reciprocal duties.
译文：在前工业时代，部落长老裁决土地纠纷，村民们之间有相互承担的义务。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1530 -->
# 六级单词 1530 · 互惠的；相互的
中文：adj. 互惠的，相互的
单词：###reciprocal###
搭配：###a reciprocal agreement### 互惠协议
例句：In preindustrial times, tribal elders settled disputes over tracts of land, and villagers were bound by ###reciprocal### duties.
译文：在前工业时代，部落长老裁决土地纠纷，村民们之间有相互承担的义务。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1779 -->
# 六级单词 1779 · 饱和
中文：n. 饱和（状态）；浸透
单词：###saturation###
搭配：###market saturation### 市场饱和
例句：Once a market reaches ###saturation###, firms must surpass rivals with a new tactic, or their profits stagnate.
译文：一旦市场饱和，企业就必须用新策略超越对手，否则利润就会停滞。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w2471 -->
# 六级单词 2471 · 超过
中文：v. 超越，凌驾，胜过；超过
单词：###surpass###
搭配：###surpass expectations### 超出预期
例句：Once a market reaches saturation, firms must ###surpass### rivals with a new tactic, or their profits stagnate.
译文：一旦市场饱和，企业就必须用新策略超越对手，否则利润就会停滞。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w2472 -->
# 六级单词 2472 · 策略，手段
中文：n. 策略，战术；手段
单词：###tactic###
搭配：###a delaying tactic### 拖延战术
例句：Once a market reaches saturation, firms must surpass rivals with a new ###tactic###, or their profits stagnate.
译文：一旦市场饱和，企业就必须用新策略超越对手，否则利润就会停滞。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w2473 -->
# 六级单词 2473 · 停滞
中文：v. (使)淤塞，(使)停滞，(使)沉滞，(使)变萧条
单词：###stagnate###
搭配：###stagnate economically### 经济停滞
例句：Once a market reaches saturation, firms must surpass rivals with a new tactic, or their profits ###stagnate###.
译文：一旦市场饱和，企业就必须用新策略超越对手，否则利润就会停滞。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1458 -->
# 六级单词 1458 · 头骨
中文：n. 脑壳，头盖骨
单词：###skull###
搭配：###a skull and crossbones### 骷髅旗
例句：Why is there a ###skull### on your desk? — It's plastic, for the Halloween play. — And that spider seems to stalk me! — Relax; superstition is silly.
译文：你桌上怎么有个骷髅头？——塑料的，万圣节演出用的。——那只蜘蛛好像在跟踪我！——放松，迷信很可笑。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w2474 -->

中文：n. 蜘蛛
单词：###spider###
搭配：###a spider's web### 蜘蛛网
例句：Why is there a skull on your desk? — It's plastic, for the Halloween play. — And that ###spider### seems to stalk me! — Relax; superstition is silly.
译文：你桌上怎么有个骷髅头？——塑料的，万圣节演出用的。——那只蜘蛛好像在跟踪我！——放松，迷信很可笑。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1454 -->
# 六级单词 1454 · 跟踪；悄悄靠近；茎
中文：n. 茎，梗 vt. 悄悄地跟踪；悄悄靠近 vi. 高视阔步地走
单词：###stalk###
搭配：###stalk prey### 跟踪猎物
例句：Why is there a skull on your desk? — It's plastic, for the Halloween play. — And that spider seems to ###stalk### me! — Relax; superstition is silly.
译文：你桌上怎么有个骷髅头？——塑料的，万圣节演出用的。——那只蜘蛛好像在跟踪我！——放松，迷信很可笑。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1090 -->
# 六级单词 1090 · 迷信
中文：n. 迷信，迷信的观念习俗
单词：###superstition###
搭配：###an old superstition### 古老的迷信
例句：Why is there a skull on your desk? — It's plastic, for the Halloween play. — And that spider seems to stalk me! — Relax; ###superstition### is silly.
译文：你桌上怎么有个骷髅头？——塑料的，万圣节演出用的。——那只蜘蛛好像在跟踪我！——放松，迷信很可笑。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w2475 -->
# 六级单词 2475 · 支撑；支柱；道具
中文：v. 支撑 n. 支柱；道具
单词：###prop###
搭配：###prop up### 支撑
例句：Engineers ###propped### up the mine shaft with steel beams, since rotting timber and rusty bolts threatened its stability.
译文：工程师用钢梁撑住了矿井，因为腐烂的木料和生锈的螺栓威胁着它的稳固。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1718 -->
# 六级单词 1718 · 竖井；轴；柄
中文：n. 杆状物；竖井，坑道；车辕，车杠；轴；柄
单词：###shaft###
搭配：###a mine shaft### 矿井
例句：Engineers propped up the mine ###shaft### with steel beams, since rotting timber and rusty bolts threatened its stability.
译文：工程师用钢梁撑住了矿井，因为腐烂的木料和生锈的螺栓威胁着它的稳固。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w2476 -->
# 六级单词 2476 · 腐烂；腐烂
中文：vi. 腐烂，腐坏 n. 腐烂
单词：###rot###
搭配：###rot away### 烂掉
例句：Engineers propped up the mine shaft with steel beams, since ###rotting### timber and rusty bolts threatened its stability.
译文：工程师用钢梁撑住了矿井，因为腐烂的木料和生锈的螺栓威胁着它的稳固。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w2477 -->
# 六级单词 2477 · 生锈的；荒疏的
中文：adj. 生锈的；变迟钝的；荒疏的
单词：###rusty###
搭配：###My French is rusty.### 我的法语生疏了。
例句：Engineers propped up the mine shaft with steel beams, since rotting timber and ###rusty### bolts threatened its stability.
译文：工程师用钢梁撑住了矿井，因为腐烂的木料和生锈的螺栓威胁着它的稳固。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1808 -->
# 六级单词 1808 · 速记；简略表达
中文：n. 速记；简略表达
单词：###shorthand###
搭配：###take notes in shorthand### 用速记做笔记
例句：Before laptops, journalists took notes in ###shorthand###, often from two speakers simultaneously, and a pencil and pad would suffice as stationery.
译文：笔记本电脑出现以前，记者用速记做笔记，常常同时记两个人的发言，一支铅笔一个本子就够当文具了。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1050 -->
# 六级单词 1050 · 同时地
中文：adv. 同步地，同时地
单词：###simultaneously###
搭配：###happen simultaneously### 同时发生
例句：Before laptops, journalists took notes in shorthand, often from two speakers ###simultaneously###, and a pencil and pad would suffice as stationery.
译文：笔记本电脑出现以前，记者用速记做笔记，常常同时记两个人的发言，一支铅笔一个本子就够当文具了。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1795 -->
# 六级单词 1795 · 足够
中文：v. 足够；足够…之需，使满足
单词：###suffice###
搭配：###Suffice it to say ...### 只要说……就够了
例句：Before laptops, journalists took notes in shorthand, often from two speakers simultaneously, and a pencil and pad would ###suffice### as stationery.
译文：笔记本电脑出现以前，记者用速记做笔记，常常同时记两个人的发言，一支铅笔一个本子就够当文具了。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1526 -->
# 六级单词 1526 · 文具
中文：n. 文具
单词：###stationery###
搭配：###office stationery### 办公文具
例句：Before laptops, journalists took notes in shorthand, often from two speakers simultaneously, and a pencil and pad would suffice as ###stationery###.
译文：笔记本电脑出现以前，记者用速记做笔记，常常同时记两个人的发言，一支铅笔一个本子就够当文具了。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1517 -->
# 六级单词 1517 · 精明的
中文：adj. 明智的，精明的；狡狯的，明辨的
单词：###shrewd###
搭配：###a shrewd businessman### 精明的商人
例句：You're ###shrewd### and unbiased, so could you mediate between my roommates and help them reconcile?
译文：你既精明又公正，能不能在我两个室友之间调解一下，帮他们和好？
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1518 -->
# 六级单词 1518 · 公正的，无偏见的
中文：adj. 公正的；无偏见的
单词：###unbiased###
搭配：###an unbiased opinion### 公正的意见
例句：You're shrewd and ###unbiased###, so could you mediate between my roommates and help them reconcile?
译文：你既精明又公正，能不能在我两个室友之间调解一下，帮他们和好？
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1519 -->
# 六级单词 1519 · 调解
中文：v. 调停，斡旋；调解
单词：###mediate###
搭配：###mediate between### 在……之间调解
例句：You're shrewd and unbiased, so could you ###mediate### between my roommates and help them reconcile?
译文：你既精明又公正，能不能在我两个室友之间调解一下，帮他们和好？
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1520 -->
# 六级单词 1520 · 使和好，调和
中文：v. 使和解，使和好；调和，使一致
单词：###reconcile###
搭配：###be reconciled with### 与……和好
例句：You're shrewd and unbiased, so could you mediate between my roommates and help them ###reconcile###?
译文：你既精明又公正，能不能在我两个室友之间调解一下，帮他们和好？
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1758 -->
# 六级单词 1758 · 繁殖；传播
中文：n. 繁殖；传播；蔓延
单词：###propagation###
搭配：###the propagation of ideas### 思想的传播
例句：The weed's ###propagation### relies on seeds carried by the wind, and because it is robust and spreads randomly, farmers can hardly repel it.
译文：这种杂草靠风带着种子繁殖，又因为生命力强、到处乱长，农民们很难挡住它。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1792 -->
# 六级单词 1792 · 击退；排斥
中文：v. 逐退，驱逐；使厌恶，使反感；击退；排斥
单词：###repel###
搭配：###repel insects### 驱虫
例句：The weed's propagation relies on seeds carried by the wind, and because it is robust and spreads randomly, farmers can hardly ###repel### it.
译文：这种杂草靠风带着种子繁殖，又因为生命力强、到处乱长，农民们很难挡住它。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1780 -->
# 六级单词 1780 · 强健的；稳健的
中文：adj. 强健的，健康的，充满活力的；稳健的
单词：###robust###
搭配：###robust growth### 强劲增长
例句：The weed's propagation relies on seeds carried by the wind, and because it is ###robust### and spreads randomly, farmers can hardly repel it.
译文：这种杂草靠风带着种子繁殖，又因为生命力强、到处乱长，农民们很难挡住它。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1049 -->
# 六级单词 1049 · 随机地
中文：adv. 随机地
单词：###randomly###
搭配：###randomly selected### 随机选取的
例句：The weed's propagation relies on seeds carried by the wind, and because it is robust and spreads ###randomly###, farmers can hardly repel it.
译文：这种杂草靠风带着种子繁殖，又因为生命力强、到处乱长，农民们很难挡住它。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1580 -->
# 六级单词 1580 · 蜗牛
中文：n. 蜗牛；行动缓慢的人
单词：###snail###
搭配：###at a snail's pace### 慢吞吞地
例句：Look, a ###snail### on that tree stump! — It's tangled in some grass. — Don't stab at it with your stick! — I won't; I'm just watching.
译文：快看，树桩上有只蜗牛！——它被草缠住了。——别用棍子戳它！——不会的，我就看看。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1412 -->
# 六级单词 1412 · 树桩；难住
中文：n. 树桩，残株；残余部分 v. 难住
单词：###stump###
搭配：###be stumped by### 被……难住
例句：Look, a snail on that tree ###stump###! — It's tangled in some grass. — Don't stab at it with your stick! — I won't; I'm just watching.
译文：快看，树桩上有只蜗牛！——它被草缠住了。——别用棍子戳它！——不会的，我就看看。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

# 六级单词 1774 · 后退；减弱
中文：v. 后退，退去；减弱
单词：###recede###
搭配：###recede into the distance### 渐渐远去
例句：As the floodwaters ###recede###, villagers return to salvage remnants of their homes, though the smell of rotten crops lingers.
译文：洪水退去后，村民们回来抢救家里残存的东西，可庄稼腐烂的气味仍久久不散。

例句：In the formula Na2SO4, each ###subscript### gives the number of atoms of sodium, sulphur or oxygen, and above a threshold this salt harms crops.
译文：在化学式 Na2SO4 中，每个下标表示钠、硫或氧的原子个数；超过一定限度，这种盐会损害庄稼。

# 六级单词 1498 · 梯田；平台；排屋
中文：n. 梯田，台地；(梯级宽且矮的)台阶(如看台)；排房(设计相同的一排房屋)；平台
单词：###terrace###
搭配：###a roof terrace### 屋顶露台
例句：The seaside village, with its shuttered cottages and narrow ###terraces###, is reminiscent of the 1950s.
译文：这个海边村庄有着关着百叶窗的小屋和窄窄的梯台，让人想起上世纪 50 年代。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w2492 -->
# 六级单词 2492 · 使人想起的
中文：adj. 使人想起的
单词：###reminiscent###
搭配：###be reminiscent of### 使人想起
例句：The seaside village, with its shuttered cottages and narrow terraces, is ###reminiscent### of the 1950s.
译文：这个海边村庄有着关着百叶窗的小屋和窄窄的梯台，让人想起上世纪 50 年代。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1830 -->
# 六级单词 1830 · 一致地；均匀地
中文：adv. 相同地；一贯；一致地；均匀地
单词：###uniformly###
搭配：###uniformly distributed### 均匀分布的
例句：Since seas do not rise ###uniformly###, some islands may soon be submerged, leaving virgin forests a temporal window too short for species to adapt.
译文：由于海平面上升并不均匀，有些岛屿可能很快被淹没，留给原始森林的时间窗口太短，物种来不及适应。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w2493 -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1835 -->
# 六级单词 1835 · 制服；抑制
中文：v. 使屈服，征服；制服；抑制
单词：###subdue###
搭配：###subdue a fire### 扑灭火
例句：How did police ###subdue### the robber? — With a stun gun. — Where was he hiding? — In an air vent, caught in a tangle of wires!
译文：警察是怎么制服劫匪的？——用电击枪。——他藏在哪儿？——在通风管道里，被一团电线缠住了！
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1815 -->

搭配：###a stun gun### 电击枪
例句：How did police subdue the robber? — With a ###stun### gun. — Where was he hiding? — In an air vent, caught in a tangle of wires!
译文：警察是怎么制服劫匪的？——用电击枪。——他藏在哪儿？——在通风管道里，被一团电线缠住了！

例句：How did police subdue the robber? — With a stun gun. — Where was he hiding? — In an air ###vent###, caught in a tangle of wires!
译文：警察是怎么制服劫匪的？——用电击枪。——他藏在哪儿？——在通风管道里，被一团电线缠住了！
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1824 -->

搭配：###in a tangle### 乱成一团

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1414 -->
# 六级单词 1414 · 边缘；轮辋
中文：n. (圆形物的)外缘，边缘；轮辋
单词：###rim###
搭配：###the rim of a glass### 杯沿
例句：Mechanics inspect the ###rim### of each wheel, because uneven wear can cause vibration when the wheels rotate at speed.
译文：修理工会检查每个车轮的轮辋，因为磨损不均会在车轮高速转动时引起振动。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1755 -->

搭配：###uneven distribution### 分配不均
例句：Mechanics inspect the rim of each wheel, because ###uneven### wear can cause vibration when the wheels rotate at speed.
译文：修理工会检查每个车轮的轮辋，因为磨损不均会在车轮高速转动时引起振动。

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w2495 -->

搭配：###vibration damage### 振动损伤
例句：Mechanics inspect the rim of each wheel, because uneven wear can cause ###vibration### when the wheels rotate at speed.
译文：修理工会检查每个车轮的轮辋，因为磨损不均会在车轮高速转动时引起振动。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w2496 -->

搭配：###rotate crops### 轮作

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w2497 -->
# 六级单词 2497 · 静电；杂音；静止的
中文：adj. 静的；静态的；静止的 n. 静电；杂音
单词：###static###
搭配：###static electricity### 静电
例句：Why's the radio full of ###static###? — Your phone is vibrating beside it. — Oh! And wedge some paper under that 40-watt lamp; it shakes.
译文：收音机怎么全是杂音？——你的手机在旁边振动。——哦！还有，那盏 40 瓦的台灯会晃，在底下塞点纸吧。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w2498 -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1556 -->

例句：Laws once enforced racial ###segregation###, and activists vowed to unify the nation, a struggle that thereafter inspired movements worldwide.

搭配：###a people's tribune### 人民的代言人
例句：In ancient Rome, a ###tribune### could wield real power to shield the poor from the vices of corrupt officials, and therein lay his importance.

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1859 -->

搭配：###a skilled weaver### 熟练的织工
例句：Who's that ###weaver###? — An old craftsman with white whiskers. — He seems so worldly. — He's travelled everywhere, and his rugs taper into fine fringes.
译文：那位织工是谁？——一位留着白胡须的老手艺人。——他看起来见多识广。——他走遍了各地，他织的毯子边缘渐渐收细成精致的流苏。

搭配：###in a whirl### 头昏脑涨
例句：Look how the dancers ###whirl###! — And the music has such tranquility. — The plot thickens in act two. — Far better than that wretched show last week!
译文：看那些舞者转得多快！——音乐还那么宁静。——第二幕剧情更加扑朔迷离。——比上周那场糟糕的演出好多了！

搭配：###The plot thickens.### 情节变得复杂。
例句：Look how the dancers whirl! — And the music has such tranquility. — The plot ###thickens### in act two. — Far better than that wretched show last week!
译文：看那些舞者转得多快！——音乐还那么宁静。——第二幕剧情更加扑朔迷离。——比上周那场糟糕的演出好多了！
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1435 -->
# 六级单词 1435 · 可怜的；糟糕的
中文：adj. 可怜的，悲惨的；恶劣的，令人讨厌的；糟糕的
单词：###wretched###
搭配：###wretched weather### 糟透的天气
例句：Look how the dancers whirl! — And the music has such tranquility. — The plot thickens in act two. — Far better than that ###wretched### show last week!
译文：看那些舞者转得多快！——音乐还那么宁静。——第二幕剧情更加扑朔迷离。——比上周那场糟糕的演出好多了！
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1797 -->
# 六级单词 1797 · 同时发生的
中文：adj. 同时发生的，同时做出的，同时的
单词：###simultaneous###
搭配：###simultaneous interpretation### 同声传译
例句：After two ###simultaneous### blasts, authorities tried to suppress panic, but the suppression of news had the undesirable effect of fuelling rumours.
译文：两起爆炸同时发生后，当局试图压制恐慌，但封锁消息反而助长了谣言，适得其反。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1419 -->

搭配：###suppress a smile### 忍住笑

译文：两起爆炸同时发生后，当局试图压制恐慌，但封锁消息反而助长了谣言，适得其反。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1102 -->
# 六级单词 1102 · 松弛的；萧条的；淡季

搭配：###slack season### 淡季
例句：###Slack### demand forced the factory to slash prices, unfinished workpieces piled up, and workers were put on standby.
译文：需求疲软迫使工厂大幅降价，未完成的工件越堆越多，工人们被安排待命。

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1633 -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1546 -->
# 六级单词 1546 · 势利小人
中文：n. 势利小人
单词：###snob###
搭配：###a wine snob### 自命懂酒的人
例句：A ###snob### judges people by their clothes, but such snobbish views ignore that thrift, not spending, builds wealth, and they strangle genuine friendship.
译文：势利的人以衣着论人，但这种势利的看法忽视了一点：积累财富靠的是节俭而不是花钱，而且它会扼杀真正的友谊。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

中文：v. 伸开；蔓延；伸开四肢 n. 无序扩展
单词：###sprawl###
搭配：###urban sprawl### 城市无序扩张
例句：As cities ###sprawl### outward, watery wetlands are drained to make room for ultra-modern malls, leaving a stark landscape.
译文：随着城市向外蔓延，水汪汪的湿地被抽干，为超现代的商场腾地方，只留下一片荒凉的景象。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1137 -->
# 六级单词 1137 · 使搁浅；使困住；缕

单词：###strand###
搭配：###be stranded### 被困
例句：A sudden storm ###stranded### climbers on the mountain, and rescuers trekked for hours through thorn bushes, a thorny task in the dark.
译文：一场突如其来的风暴把登山者困在山上，救援人员在荆棘丛中跋涉了好几个小时，在黑暗中这可是件棘手的事。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1502 -->
# 六级单词 1502 · 战术；策略
中文：n. 战术，兵法；策略
单词：###tactics###
搭配：###sales tactics### 销售手段
例句：Advertisers use clever ###tactics### to seduce consumers with promises of unlimited data, but contracts with hidden clauses may be void in court.
译文：广告商用巧妙的手段，以“流量无限”的承诺引诱消费者，但带有隐藏条款的合同在法庭上可能无效。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w2516 -->

<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1862 -->
# 六级单词 1862 · 扣留；拒绝给予
中文：v. 不给，拒绝；扣留；拒绝给予
单词：###withhold###
搭配：###withhold information### 隐瞒信息
例句：Some firms ###withhold### safety data from undocumented workers at plants where cattle are slaughtered, and urine tests later reveal toxic exposure.
译文：一些公司对屠宰场里的无证工人隐瞒安全数据，后来尿检才显示他们接触了有毒物质。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

搭配：###a tornado warning### 龙卷风警报
例句：The ###tornado### caused such turbulence that it toppled power lines across the terrain, proving the old warning system ineffective.
译文：龙卷风引起的气流如此剧烈，把这一带的电线都刮倒了，证明旧的预警系统不管用。

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1810 -->
# 六级单词 1810 · 隔离，分开
中文：v. 将…隔离、分开；歧视，不公平对待
单词：###segregate###
搭配：###segregate A from B### 把 A 与 B 分开
例句：That the city no longer ###segregates### schools by income signifies a more unified society, though uninformed critics doubt it.
译文：这座城市不再按收入划分学校，这标志着社会更加团结，尽管一些不明真相的批评者对此表示怀疑。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1868 -->
# 六级单词 1868 · 借口，托词
中文：n. 借口，托词
单词：###pretext###
搭配：###under the pretext of### 以……为借口
例句：Under the ###pretext### of efficiency, the developer subdivided the land into tiny plots, until the market was so saturated that the housing crisis recurred.
译文：开发商以提高效率为借口，把土地分割成一块块小地皮，结果市场饱和到了极点，住房危机再次出现。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet6-words-w1869 -->
